# Web Mode M2 (Agents and Edited Files) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Make the agents' work visible in the web mode tab: live agent nodes
in the scene and an accessible agent list, an inspector that shows what a
selected agent is doing (with interrupt, cancel and message actions), the team
task board and todo orbit, and a file drawer that lists every file the session
tree edited with a read-only CodeMirror diff, plus a hardening pass over the
items deferred from M1.

**Architecture:** Go first. `workspace.Workspace` gains the four operations
the browser needs and the TUI never had to route through it (child sessions,
per-file LSP diagnostics, teammate message, teammate interrupt), each wired
through the in-process app, the server, the client SDK and the fake. The
gateway then adds `internal/web/files.go` (edited-file aggregation across the
session tree and diffs, spec 2.7) and `internal/web/team.go` (a poller over
`agent-teams/team.json` and `agents.json` that emits deduplicated `team_state`
and `agent_runs` frames, spec 2.4). The frontend derives the agent roster from
those frames with the same rules the TUI uses (`internal/ui/model/agent_discovery.go`),
keeps the inspected agent's transcript live from the message frames it
already receives, and renders the new surfaces as glass and hologram panels.
CodeMirror 6 loads lazily and mounts inside a shadow root, because its
style injection would otherwise hit the page CSP.

**Tech Stack:** Go 1.27, `net/http`, sqlc 1.30.0, testify. Frontend: Preact 11
with `@preact/signals`, three 0.187, Vite 8, Vitest 5 (jsdom), Playwright 1.65,
`@axe-core/playwright`. New runtime dependencies (all MIT, all in the lazy
diff chunk): `@codemirror/merge`, `@codemirror/view`, `@codemirror/state`,
`@codemirror/language`, `@lezer/highlight`, `@codemirror/lang-go`,
`@codemirror/lang-javascript`, `@codemirror/lang-json`, `@codemirror/lang-css`,
`@codemirror/lang-html`, `@codemirror/lang-markdown`,
`@codemirror/lang-python`, `@codemirror/lang-yaml`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-11-21-web-mode-design.md`. Read sections
1, 2.4 to 2.7, 3.3 to 3.11, 4, 5 (the M2 rows), 6, 7 and 8 before Task 1.
The M1 plans (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-11-21-web-mode-m1.md` and
`2026-11-21-web-mode-m1-redesign.md`) show the conventions this plan follows.
Deferred items come from `/tmp/web-mode-deferred.md`; the disposition of all
84 is at the end of this plan.

Date: 2026-11-22.

## Global Constraints

- Worktree: `/home/pbtrudel/git/.crush-worktrees/web-mode-m2`, branch
  `crush/web-mode`. Work only there; absolute paths for file tools,
  `working_dir` for bash. Never touch `/home/pbtrudel/git/crush`.
- Every go command is prefixed with `GOCACHE=/tmp/crush-go-build`. CGO is
  off. There is no `task` CLI; run the underlying commands. sqlc is
  `/home/pbtrudel/go/bin/sqlc` (v1.30.0, the version the generated files
  carry). A `golangci-lint` binary sits in `/home/pbtrudel/go/bin` but is
  not part of the Check run; CI's lint job is authoritative.
- Playwright runs as
  `cd web && LD_LIBRARY_PATH=/tmp/alsa-stub npx --no-update-notifier playwright test`.
  Never commit the stub.
- CSP is `default-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data: blob:; style-src 'self';
  connect-src 'self'; object-src 'none'; frame-ancestors 'none'` and does not
  change in M2. No `style=` attributes in markup, no inline `<style>`
  elements. Setting properties through the CSSOM (`el.style.transform = ...`)
  and constructable stylesheets (`adoptedStyleSheets`) are allowed.
- CSS animations and transitions may only animate `transform`, `opacity` and
  `filter` (spec 3.9); `web/src/styles/motion.test.ts` enforces it and every
  new stylesheet is added to its `EXPECTED_SHEETS` list.
- No business logic in the gateway or the browser: every action is a
  `workspace.Workspace` call. A missing operation is added to `Workspace`
  (app, server, client, fake) before the gateway uses it (spec 2.6, 8).
- Edited files (spec 2.7): every path with history in the session and its
  child sessions; diff defaults first to latest version; payloads over 1 MB
  (`1 << 20` bytes) return `truncated: true` with only the unified diff;
  paths are validated against the session tree's history set and content is
  never read from disk.
- Stream (spec 2.4): ring 2048 frames, send queue 512, overflow close 4001,
  ping 15 s, idle close 45 s. New gateway frame types: `team_state`,
  `agent_runs`. Payloads are snake_case JSON.
- Any new wire or gateway frame is added to `fixtureEvents()` or
  `gatewayFixtureFrames()` in `internal/web/fixtures_test.go` and
  `web/src/state/__fixtures__/events.json` is regenerated with
  `CRUSH_WEB_FIXTURES=1`, never hand-edited.
- Palette (spec 3.2): void `oklch(0.12 0.05 275)`, haze `oklch(0.30 0.16 300)`,
  glass `oklch(0.16 0.05 265 / 0.70)`, glass solid `oklch(0.16 0.05 265 / 0.94)`,
  holo cyan `oklch(0.86 0.17 205)`, magenta `oklch(0.70 0.29 340)`, violet
  `oklch(0.62 0.25 295)`, teal `oklch(0.82 0.16 170)`, amber `oklch(0.83 0.17 75)`,
  text `oklch(0.95 0.02 220)`, muted `oklch(0.74 0.05 240)`. Teammates are
  cyan, subagents violet, magenta only for "needs you" and failures.
- Node statuses (spec 3.4): running (bright, slow spin), inactive (dim),
  provisioning (assembling wireframe), failed (magenta flicker plus icon),
  done (collapses into the core). Every status also has a text label.
- Eager bundle budget: `npm run budget` limit is 614,400 bytes gzip (600 KiB).
  Measured on 2026-11-22: 58,069 bytes (`index-*.js` 50,822 plus
  `index-*.css` 7,247). three.js, the cinematic flight and CodeMirror stay
  in lazy chunks; Task 18 makes the budget script fail if they leak into the
  eager set.
- New npm dependencies must carry a license from the CI allow-list
  (BSD-2-Clause, BSD-3-Clause, MIT, MIT-0, Apache-2.0, MPL-2.0, ISC,
  Unlicense, CC-BY-4.0, OFL-1.1). After `npm install`, check
  `node_modules/<pkg>/package.json` `license` for every new direct and
  transitive package (`npm ls --all --json` lists them).
- DOM order of the log, the stage section, the deck and the interrupt layer
  is unchanged. New panels are inserted after the log inside `.cine-camera`
  (inspector) and after the stage (drawer).
- Go style: gofumpt, testify `require`, `t.Parallel()`, own-line comments are
  full sentences ending in a period, log messages start with a capital
  letter, `noctx` rules from M1 (tests use `http.NewRequestWithContext`,
  cleanups that call `Shutdown` use `context.Background()`).
- TypeScript strict, existing file patterns. No em dashes in any source or
  doc line. Comments only where the reason is not obvious.
- Visual baselines under `web/e2e/visual.spec.ts-snapshots/` change with the
  M2 layout. Tasks 13 to 19 run the Playwright smoke specs only
  (`playwright test smoke agents files`); Task 20 regenerates every baseline
  deliberately.
- Each task ends with its own test commands, then the **Check run**, then one
  commit with a one-line semantic message and no trailers. A task that
  touches `web/` commits the rebuilt `internal/web/dist`.

**Check run** (every task):

```bash
cd /home/pbtrudel/git/.crush-worktrees/web-mode-m2
gofumpt -l internal main.go; GOCACHE=/tmp/crush-go-build go vet ./internal/web/... ./internal/workspace/... ./internal/agent/... ./internal/server/... ./internal/client/... ./internal/backend/... ./internal/session/...
GOCACHE=/tmp/crush-go-build go test ./internal/web/... ./internal/wire/... ./internal/workspace/... ./internal/session/... ./internal/server/... ./internal/client/... ./internal/backend/... ./internal/agent/... ./internal/agentteam/...
cd web && npm run typecheck && npm test && npm run build && npm run budget
cd .. && GOCACHE=/tmp/crush-go-build go build . && grep -c 'style=' internal/web/dist/index.html
git status --porcelain -- internal/web/dist
git diff --cached -- . ':!internal/web/dist' ':!web/package-lock.json' | grep -c $'^+.*—'
```

`gofumpt -l` must print nothing, the two `grep -c` lines must print 0 (the
em dash check runs after `git add`), and `git status` must list every dist
change you are about to commit (new hashed chunks are untracked until added).

## Review Focus

1. A subagent edits a file the Lead edited before and after it: the drawer
   shows one entry with every version in version order, the diff defaults
   to first against latest, and a `from` or `to` that is not in that file's
   history answers 400 instead of diffing something else. Pinned in Task 6
   (`TestFileDiffAcrossChildSessions`, `TestFileDiffRejectsUnknownVersion`).
2. A path the browser was never given (another session's file, `../../etc/passwd`,
   an empty path) is refused without reading disk. Pinned in Task 6
   (`TestFileDiffRejectsPathOutsideTree`).
3. A generated file of several megabytes is edited: the diff answer stays
   under 1 MiB, is cut on a line and rune boundary, and the drawer renders
   the unified fallback instead of stalling. Pinned in Task 6
   (`TestBuildDiffTruncatesLargePayload`, `TestTruncateLinesKeepsRunes`) and
   Task 18 (`renders the unified fallback when the diff is truncated`).
4. `team.json` is unreadable for a moment (a manual edit, a half-written
   copy, a deleted directory) while a tab is open: the board and the nodes
   keep the last good roster and no frame is published until it reads
   again. Pinned in Task 7 (`TestTeamWatcherKeepsLastGoodState`,
   `TestTeamWatcherMissingFiles`).
5. Live frames for the inspected agent arrive while its transcript is still
   loading, and the agent then leaves the registry (the 2 minute finished
   grace): no message is lost or duplicated, and the inspector stays open
   with a "Done" status instead of vanishing under the user. Pinned in Task
   10 (`merges live frames that arrived during the transcript fetch`) and
   Task 15 (`keeps the inspector open when the agent leaves the roster`).

### Task 1: Workspace child sessions and per-file diagnostics

Spec 2.7 aggregates edits across child sessions and shows diagnostics per
file. `ListSessions` filters children out (`WHERE parent_session_id is NULL`)
and no `Workspace` method lists them; LSP diagnostics are only exposed per
server. Both are added to `Workspace` first (spec 8).

**Files:**
- Modify: `internal/db/sql/sessions.sql` (append query)
- Regenerate: `internal/db/sessions.sql.go`, `internal/db/querier.go`, `internal/db/db.go`
- Modify: `internal/session/session.go:85-120` (interface) and add the method
- Test: `internal/session/session_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/app/resolve_session_test.go`, `internal/agent/tools/todos_test.go` (mock stubs)
- Modify: `internal/workspace/workspace.go:130-145` and `:238-243`

- Modify: `internal/workspace/app_workspace.go`, `internal/workspace/client_workspace.go:582-603`
- Test: `internal/workspace/client_workspace_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/backend/session.go`, `internal/server/endpoints.go:214-221`, `internal/server/proto.go:402-411`, `internal/client/proto.go:702-716`
- Modify: `internal/web/webtest/fake.go`

- Produces:
  - `session.Service.ListChildren(ctx context.Context, parentID string) ([]session.Session, error)`
  - `workspace.Workspace.ListChildSessions(ctx context.Context, parentID string) ([]session.Session, error)`

New fake state is only changed through setters that take `mu`: a test that writes a field while the gateway's handler goroutines read it is a data race under `-race`.

```go
func (s *service) ListChildren(ctx context.Context, parentID string) ([]Session, error) {
	rows, err := s.q.ListChildSessions(ctx, sql.NullString{String: parentID, Valid: true})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]Session, len(rows))
	for i, row := range rows {
		out[i] = s.fromDBItem(row)
		s.applyEstimatedUsageState(&out[i])
	}
	return out, nil
}
```

```go
package workspace

import (

func TestAddDiagnosticsCountsByPathAndSeverity(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	out := map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts{}
	addDiagnostics(out, map[protocol.DocumentURI][]protocol.Diagnostic{
		protocol.URIFromPath("/repo/a.go"): {
			{Severity: protocol.SeverityError},
			{Severity: protocol.SeverityWarning},
			{Severity: protocol.SeverityWarning},
		},
		"": {{Severity: protocol.SeverityError}},
	})
	// A second server reporting on the same file adds to its counts.
	addDiagnostics(out, map[protocol.DocumentURI][]protocol.Diagnostic{
		protocol.URIFromPath("/repo/a.go"): {{Severity: protocol.SeverityHint}},
	})
	require.Equal(t, map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts{
		"/repo/a.go": {Error: 1, Warning: 2, Hint: 1},
	}, out)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement the workspace side**

`internal/workspace/diagnostics.go`:

```go
package workspace

import (
	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/lsp"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/x/powernap/pkg/lsp/protocol"
)

// addDiagnostics adds one server's diagnostics to out, keyed by file path.
// URIs that do not name a file are skipped.
func addDiagnostics(out map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts, diags map[protocol.DocumentURI][]protocol.Diagnostic) {
	for uri, list := range diags {
		path, err := uri.Path()
		if err != nil || path == "" {
			continue
		}
		counts := out[path]
		for _, d := range list {
			countSeverity(&counts, d.Severity)
		}
		out[path] = counts
	}
}

func countSeverity(c *lsp.DiagnosticCounts, s protocol.DiagnosticSeverity) {
	switch s {
	case protocol.SeverityError:
		c.Error++
	case protocol.SeverityWarning:
		c.Warning++
	case protocol.SeverityInformation:
		c.Information++
	case protocol.SeverityHint:
		c.Hint++
	}
}
```

Replace the inline severity switch in `ClientWorkspace.LSPGetDiagnosticCounts`

```go
	ListChildSessions(ctx context.Context, parentID string) ([]session.Session, error)
```

Under `// LSP` after `LSPGetDiagnosticCounts`:

`app_workspace.go`:

```go
func (w *AppWorkspace) ListChildSessions(ctx context.Context, parentID string) ([]session.Session, error) {
	return w.app.Sessions.ListChildren(ctx, parentID)
}

func (w *AppWorkspace) LSPDiagnosticsByFile(context.Context) map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts {
	out := map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts{}
	for _, state := range app.GetLSPStates() {
		if state.Client != nil {
			addDiagnostics(out, state.Client.GetDiagnostics())
		}
	}
	return out
}
```

`client_workspace.go`:

```go
func (w *ClientWorkspace) ListChildSessions(ctx context.Context, parentID string) ([]session.Session, error) {
	list, err := w.client.ListChildSessions(ctx, w.workspaceID(), parentID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	out := make([]session.Session, len(list))
	for i, s := range list {
		out[i] = protoToSession(s)
	}
	return out, nil
}

// LSPDiagnosticsByFile asks every server for its diagnostics. A server
// that fails to answer is left out rather than failing the whole list.
func (w *ClientWorkspace) LSPDiagnosticsByFile(ctx context.Context) map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts {
	out := map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts{}
	states, err := w.client.GetLSPs(ctx, w.workspaceID())
	if err != nil {
		return out
	}
	for name := range states {
		diags, err := w.client.GetLSPDiagnostics(ctx, w.workspaceID(), name)
		if err != nil {
			continue
		}
		addDiagnostics(out, diags)
	}
	return out
}
```

`internal/client/proto.go`, after `ListSessionHistoryFiles`:

```go
// ListChildSessions returns the sessions a session spawned.
func (c *Client) ListChildSessions(ctx context.Context, id string, sessionID string) ([]proto.Session, error) {
	rsp, err := c.get(ctx, fmt.Sprintf("/workspaces/%s/sessions/%s/children", id, sessionID), nil, nil)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get child sessions: %w", err)
	}
	defer rsp.Body.Close()
	if rsp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to get child sessions: status code %d", rsp.StatusCode)
	}
	var sessions []proto.Session
	if err := json.NewDecoder(rsp.Body).Decode(&sessions); err != nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("failed to decode child sessions: %w", err)
	}
	return sessions, nil
}
```

```go
// handleGetWorkspaceSessionChildren returns the sessions a session spawned.
func (c *controllerV1) handleGetWorkspaceSessionChildren(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("id")
	sid := r.PathValue("sid")
	children, err := c.backend.ListChildSessions(r.Context(), id, sid)
	if err != nil {
		c.handleError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	result := make([]proto.Session, len(children))
	for i, s := range children {
		result[i] = wire.SessionToProto(s)
	}
	jsonEncode(w, result)
}
```

In `internal/web/webtest/fake.go` add unexported fields
`history map[string][]history.File` (initialized in `NewFake`) and
`diagnostics map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts`, then:

```go
// AddSession stores a session, such as a child session with a parent.
func (f *Fake) AddSession(s session.Session) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	f.sessions[s.ID] = s
	f.mu.Unlock()
}

// AddHistory stores one file version for ListSessionHistory.
func (f *Fake) AddHistory(file history.File) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	f.history[file.SessionID] = append(f.history[file.SessionID], file)
	f.mu.Unlock()
}

func (f *Fake) ListChildSessions(_ context.Context, parentID string) ([]session.Session, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	var out []session.Session
	for _, s := range f.sessions {
		if s.ParentSessionID == parentID {
			out = append(out, s)
		}
	}
	slices.SortFunc(out, func(a, b session.Session) int {
		return cmp.Or(cmp.Compare(a.CreatedAt, b.CreatedAt), cmp.Compare(a.ID, b.ID))
	})
	return out, nil
}

func (f *Fake) ListSessionHistory(_ context.Context, sessionID string) ([]history.File, error) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return append([]history.File(nil), f.history[sessionID]...), nil
}

// SetDiagnostics sets what LSPDiagnosticsByFile returns.
func (f *Fake) SetDiagnostics(m map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts) {
	f.mu.Lock()
	f.diagnostics = maps.Clone(m)
	f.mu.Unlock()
}

func (f *Fake) LSPDiagnosticsByFile(context.Context) map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts {
	f.mu.Lock()
	defer f.mu.Unlock()
	return maps.Clone(f.diagnostics)
}
```

### Task 2: Workspace teammate message and interrupt

The inspector's teammate actions (spec 3.8: "actions (cancel, message for
teammates)") have no `Workspace` operation: the TUI has neither. Interrupt
must go through the Team service, which is the only path to a teammate's
detached run (`stopSessionTree` in `internal/agent/session_tree.go` says
so). Subagents need nothing new: `AgentCancel(childSessionID)` already stops
a dispatched subagent (`coordinator.Cancel`).

- Create: `internal/agent/team_control.go`, `internal/agent/team_control_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/workspace/workspace.go`, `app_workspace.go`, `client_workspace.go`
- Create: `internal/proto/team.go`
- Create: `internal/backend/team.go`

- Test: `internal/workspace/client_workspace_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/web/webtest/fake.go`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `agentteam.LeadName = "lead"` (exported alias of `leadName`)
  - `agent.ErrAgentTeamsDisabled error`
  - `agent.TeamController` with `TeamSendMessage(ctx context.Context, sessionID, member, text string) error` and `TeamInterrupt(ctx context.Context, sessionID, member string) error`
  - `workspace.Workspace.TeamSendMessage(ctx context.Context, sessionID, member, text string) error`
  - `workspace.Workspace.TeamInterrupt(ctx context.Context, sessionID, member string) error`
  - `proto.TeamMessageRequest{SessionID string "session_id"; Text string "text"}`, `proto.TeamInterruptRequest{SessionID string "session_id"}`
  - Server routes `POST /v1/workspaces/{id}/team/members/{member}/message` and `.../interrupt`, 200 on success, 409 for `ErrAgentTeamsDisabled`, 400 for an `agentteam.Error`
  - `(*webtest.Fake).SetTeamErr(err error)` (returned by both calls) and call log entries `TeamSendMessage:<sid>:<member>:<text>`, `TeamInterrupt:<sid>:<member>`
  - `sessionID` is the Lead session the browser is following; the Team service scopes the member to that session's tree.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `internal/agentteam/team.go`, next to `leadName`:

```go
// LeadName is the roster name of the Team Lead. Frontends that steer a
// team on the user's behalf act as the Lead.
const LeadName = leadName
```

`internal/agent/team_control.go`:

```go
package agent

import (
	"context"
	"errors"

	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/agentteam"
)

// ErrAgentTeamsDisabled reports a team action while Agent Teams is off.
var ErrAgentTeamsDisabled = errors.New("agent teams are not enabled")

// TeamController is implemented by coordinators that let a frontend steer
// a session's teammates the way the Lead's own tools do.
type TeamController interface {
	// TeamSendMessage sends text to member as the Lead of sessionID's
	// team and wakes the member when it is inactive.
	TeamSendMessage(ctx context.Context, sessionID, member, text string) error
	// TeamInterrupt stops member's current turn.
	TeamInterrupt(ctx context.Context, sessionID, member string) error
}

var _ TeamController = (*coordinator)(nil)

func (c *coordinator) TeamSendMessage(ctx context.Context, sessionID, member, text string) error {
	// Read the service once, so the nil check and the call use the same one.
	return sendTeamMessage(ctx, c.team, sessionID, member, text)
}

func (c *coordinator) TeamInterrupt(_ context.Context, sessionID, member string) error {
	return interruptTeammate(c.team, sessionID, member)
}

// sendTeamMessage speaks as the Lead: the user steers the team through the
// Lead, and the Team service has no other sender a teammate would trust.
func sendTeamMessage(ctx context.Context, team *agentteam.Service, sessionID, member, text string) error {
	if team == nil {
		return ErrAgentTeamsDisabled
	}
	// The send may start the member's turn, which must outlive the request.
	return team.SendMessage(context.WithoutCancel(ctx), agentteam.LeadName, sessionID, agentteam.SendRequest{
		Target:  member,
		Message: text,
	})
}

func interruptTeammate(team *agentteam.Service, sessionID, member string) error {
	if team == nil {
		return ErrAgentTeamsDisabled
	}
	_, err := team.Interrupt(agentteam.LeadName, sessionID, member)
	return err
}
```

```go
package proto

// TeamMessageRequest sends a message to a teammate as the Lead of a
// session's Agent Team.
type TeamMessageRequest struct {
	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
	Text      string `json:"text"`
}

// TeamInterruptRequest stops a teammate's current turn.
type TeamInterruptRequest struct {
	SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
}
```

`internal/backend/team.go`:

```go
package backend

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/agent"
)

// TeamSendMessage sends text to a teammate of sessionID's team as its Lead.
func (b *Backend) TeamSendMessage(ctx context.Context, workspaceID, sessionID, member, text string) error {
	tc, err := b.teamController(workspaceID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return tc.TeamSendMessage(ctx, sessionID, member, text)
}

// TeamInterrupt stops a teammate's current turn.
func (b *Backend) TeamInterrupt(ctx context.Context, workspaceID, sessionID, member string) error {
	tc, err := b.teamController(workspaceID)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return tc.TeamInterrupt(ctx, sessionID, member)
}

func (b *Backend) teamController(workspaceID string) (agent.TeamController, error) {
	ws, err := b.GetWorkspace(workspaceID)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	if ws.AgentCoordinator == nil {
		return nil, ErrAgentNotInitialized
	}
	tc, ok := ws.AgentCoordinator.(agent.TeamController)
	if !ok {
		return nil, agent.ErrAgentTeamsDisabled
	}
	return tc, nil
}
```

`internal/server/proto.go`:

```go
// handlePostWorkspaceTeamMemberMessage sends a message to a teammate.
func (c *controllerV1) handlePostWorkspaceTeamMemberMessage(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req proto.TeamMessageRequest
	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
		c.server.logError(r, "Failed to decode request", "error", err)
		jsonError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "failed to decode request")
		return
	}
	if err := c.backend.TeamSendMessage(r.Context(), r.PathValue("id"), req.SessionID, r.PathValue("member"), req.Text); err != nil {
		c.handleError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
}

// handlePostWorkspaceTeamMemberInterrupt stops a teammate's current turn.
func (c *controllerV1) handlePostWorkspaceTeamMemberInterrupt(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var req proto.TeamInterruptRequest
	if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
		c.server.logError(r, "Failed to decode request", "error", err)
		jsonError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "failed to decode request")
		return
	}
	if err := c.backend.TeamInterrupt(r.Context(), r.PathValue("id"), req.SessionID, r.PathValue("member")); err != nil {
		c.handleError(w, r, err)
		return
	}
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusOK)
}
```

`ErrClientNotAttached` comment there).

```go
// TeamSendMessage sends a message to a teammate as the Lead of sessionID's
// team.
func (c *Client) TeamSendMessage(ctx context.Context, id, sessionID, member, text string) error {
	body := jsonBody(proto.TeamMessageRequest{SessionID: sessionID, Text: text})
	rsp, err := c.post(ctx, fmt.Sprintf("/workspaces/%s/team/members/%s/message", id, url.PathEscape(member)), nil, body, http.Header{"Content-Type": []string{"application/json"}})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to message teammate: %w", err)
	}
	defer rsp.Body.Close()
	if rsp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to message teammate: status code %d", rsp.StatusCode)
	}
	return nil
}

// TeamInterrupt stops a teammate's current turn.
func (c *Client) TeamInterrupt(ctx context.Context, id, sessionID, member string) error {
	body := jsonBody(proto.TeamInterruptRequest{SessionID: sessionID})
	rsp, err := c.post(ctx, fmt.Sprintf("/workspaces/%s/team/members/%s/interrupt", id, url.PathEscape(member)), nil, body, http.Header{"Content-Type": []string{"application/json"}})
	if err != nil {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to interrupt teammate: %w", err)
	}
	defer rsp.Body.Close()
	if rsp.StatusCode != http.StatusOK {
		return fmt.Errorf("failed to interrupt teammate: status code %d", rsp.StatusCode)
	}
	return nil
}
```

```go
func TestClientWorkspaceSubscribeAfterShutdownIsClosed(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	w := &ClientWorkspace{}
	w.closeFan()
	ch := w.SubscribeEvents(t.Context())
	select {
	case _, open := <-ch:
		require.False(t, open, "a subscription after shutdown must be closed at once")
	case <-time.After(time.Second):
		t.Fatal("subscription after shutdown stayed open")
	}
}

func TestDropWarnerThrottles(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	var d dropWarner
	start := time.Unix(1000, 0)
	total, warn := d.drop(start)
	require.True(t, warn)
	require.EqualValues(t, 1, total)
	_, warn = d.drop(start.Add(time.Second))
	require.False(t, warn, "a second drop inside the interval stays quiet")
	total, warn = d.drop(start.Add(dropWarnInterval + time.Second))
	require.True(t, warn)
	require.EqualValues(t, 3, total, "the warning carries every drop since the start")
}
```

`internal/workspace/droplog.go`:

```go
package workspace

import (
	"sync"
	"time"
)

// dropWarnInterval spaces the warnings about events dropped for a slow
// subscriber, like the pubsub broker's own drop warning.
const dropWarnInterval = 5 * time.Second

// dropWarner counts dropped events and says when a warning is due. The
// count is cumulative, so one warning still says how much was lost.
type dropWarner struct {
	mu    sync.Mutex
	total int64
	last  time.Time
}

func (d *dropWarner) drop(now time.Time) (total int64, warn bool) {
	d.mu.Lock()
	defer d.mu.Unlock()
	d.total++
	if !d.last.IsZero() && now.Sub(d.last) < dropWarnInterval {
		return d.total, false
	}
	d.last = now
	return d.total, true
}
```

In `ClientWorkspace` add `fanDrops dropWarner` and `fanClosed bool` (guarded

Move the fan close loop out of `Shutdown` into:

```go
// closeFan closes every non-TUI subscription and refuses new ones.
func (w *ClientWorkspace) closeFan() {
	w.fanMu.Lock()
	defer w.fanMu.Unlock()
	w.fanClosed = true
	for ch := range w.fanSubs {
		delete(w.fanSubs, ch)
		close(ch)
	}
}
```

and call `w.closeFan()` where the loop was. Replace
`sessionToProto(sess)` in `SaveSession` with `wire.SessionToProto(sess)`
(import `github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/wire`; `wire` does not
import `workspace`, so there is no cycle) and delete the private
`sessionToProto` and `todosToProto`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`auth.go`, `originAllowed`:

```go
// originAllowed checks Origin. Browsers send it on every request that can
// change state (any fetch that is not a GET or HEAD, and the WebSocket
// upgrade), so a state-changing request without one did not come from the
// tab and is refused. Reads without an Origin stay allowed: SameSite=Strict
// and the Host guard already cover them.
func (g *Gateway) originAllowed(r *http.Request) bool {
	origin := r.Header.Get("Origin")
	if origin == "" {
		return r.Method == http.MethodGet || r.Method == http.MethodHead
	}
	u, err := url.Parse(origin)
	if err != nil {
		return false
	}
	return u.Scheme == "http" && g.hostAllowed(u.Host) && u.Host == r.Host
}
```

`api.go`:

```go
// writeInternal answers a failed workspace call without its error text,
// which can carry file paths, SQL or provider details, and logs it instead.
func writeInternal(w http.ResponseWriter, code string, err error) {
	slog.Warn("Web API call failed", "code", code, "error", err)
	writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, code, "the workspace could not complete the request")
}

// isNotFound reports a lookup of something the workspace does not have.
func isNotFound(err error) bool {
	return errors.Is(err, sql.ErrNoRows)
}
```

Replace every `writeError(w, http.StatusInternalServerError, X, err.Error())`
with `writeInternal(w, X, err)`. In `handlePrompt` replace the two
`bad_attachment` messages with `"an attachment could not be read"`.
`handleMessages` becomes:

```go
func (g *Gateway) handleMessages(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	sid := r.PathValue("sid")
	if _, err := g.ws.GetSession(r.Context(), sid); err != nil {
		if isNotFound(err) {
			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "unknown_session", "no such session")
			return
		}
		writeInternal(w, "get_session", err)
		return
	}
	msgs, err := g.ws.ListMessages(r.Context(), sid)
	if err != nil {
		writeInternal(w, "list_messages", err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, entities(msgs))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the ring**

`hub` keeps `ring []frame` plus `start int` (index of the oldest frame):

```go
func (h *hub) publish(typ string, raw json.RawMessage) frame {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	h.seq++
	f := frame{Seq: h.seq, Type: typ, Payload: raw}
	// A head index instead of a shift: publishing stays O(1) under the
	// lock however full the ring is.
	if len(h.ring) < ringSize {
		h.ring = append(h.ring, f)
	} else {
		h.ring[h.start] = f
		h.start = (h.start + 1) % ringSize
	}
	for c := range h.subs {
		select {
		case c.ch <- f:
		default:
			c.once.Do(func() { close(c.overflow) })
			delete(h.subs, c)
		}
	}
	return f
}

// frames returns the ring oldest first. The caller holds no lock.
func (h *hub) frames() []frame {
	h.mu.Lock()
	defer h.mu.Unlock()
	return h.orderedLocked()
}

func (h *hub) orderedLocked() []frame {
	out := make([]frame, 0, len(h.ring))
	out = append(out, h.ring[h.start:]...)
	return append(out, h.ring[:h.start]...)
}
```

In `attach`, the replay part becomes:

```go
	ordered := h.orderedLocked()
	if len(ordered) == 0 || ordered[0].Seq > uint64(since)+1 {
		return c, nil, true, h.seq
	}
	// Seqs are contiguous, so the replay starts at a computed offset.
	offset := int(uint64(since) + 1 - ordered[0].Seq)
	return c, append([]frame(nil), ordered[offset:]...), false, h.seq
```

`Options` gains:

```go
	idleTimeout time.Duration
```

`Start` sets `g.idle = cmp.Or(opts.idleTimeout, idleTimeout)`, and
`readLoop` uses `g.idle`. In `pump`, the closed-channel branch becomes:

```go
		case ev, ok := <-events:
			if !ok {
				// The workspace stopped sending events, but the terminal
				// can still switch sessions; keep following it.
				events = nil
				continue
			}
```

`pending.observe`, notification case:

```go

		if e.Payload.ToolCallID == "" {
			break
		}
```

`webtest/fake.go`:

```go
// CloseSubscribers closes every SubscribeEvents channel, the way a
// workspace that shut down does.
func (f *Fake) CloseSubscribers() {
	f.subsMu.Lock()
	subs := make([]*subscriber, 0, len(f.subs))
	for s := range f.subs {
		subs = append(subs, s)
		delete(f.subs, s)
	}
	f.subsMu.Unlock()
	for _, s := range subs {
		s.mu.Lock()
		if !s.closed {
			s.closed = true
			close(s.ch)
		}
		s.mu.Unlock()
	}
}
```

The `SubscribeEvents` cleanup goroutine must skip the close when
`s.closed` is already true (wrap its `close(s.ch)` in `if !s.closed`). Give
`Calls` the doc comment `// Calls is guarded by mu; read it through CallLog.`

### Task 6: Gateway edited files and diffs

Spec 2.7 and the `internal/web/files.go` row of spec 2.1. Versions in
`internal/history` are numbered per path across every session
(`CreateVersion` reads `ListFilesByPath`), so sorting a path's versions from
the whole session tree by version number interleaves the Lead's and the
subagents' edits in the order they happened.

**Files:**
- Create: `internal/web/files.go`, `internal/web/files_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/web/api.go:29-48` (routes)
- Modify: `internal/web/encode.go:35-43` (file frames without content)
- Modify: `internal/web/snapshot.go:37-106` (`Files`)
- Modify: `internal/web/fixtures_test.go` (`file` event, snapshot `files`)
- Modify: `internal/web/auth_test.go` (`protectedRoutes`)
- Regenerate: `web/src/state/__fixtures__/events.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Workspace.ListChildSessions`, `Workspace.ListSessionHistory`, `Workspace.LSPDiagnosticsByFile` (Task 1); `writeInternal` (Task 4).
- Produces:
  - `GET /api/sessions/{sid}/files` answering `[]FileSummary`, newest `updated_at` first
  - `GET /api/sessions/{sid}/files/diff?path=<abs>&from=<version>&to=<version>` answering `FileDiff`; 400 `missing_path`, 400 `bad_version`, 404 `unknown_path`
  - `type FileSummary struct { Path string "path"; Versions int "versions"; VersionIDs []int64 "version_ids"; Additions int "additions"; Removals int "removals"; Diagnostics FileDiagnostics "diagnostics"; UpdatedAt int64 "updated_at"; SessionIDs []string "session_ids" }`
  - `type FileDiagnostics struct { Error int "error"; Warning int "warning" }`
  - `type FileDiff struct { Path string "path"; From int64 "from"; To int64 "to"; Old string "old"; New string "new"; Unified string "unified"; Language string "language"; Truncated bool "truncated" }`
  - `Snapshot.Files []FileSummary "files"` for the snapshot's session
  - `file` frames keep `id`, `session_id`, `path`, `version`, `created_at`, `updated_at` and drop `content`
  - `languageFor(path string) string` values: `go`, `typescript`, `tsx`, `javascript`, `jsx`, `json`, `css`, `html`, `markdown`, `python`, `yaml`, `text`

```go
package web

import (
	"encoding/json"
	"net/http"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/history"

)

// seedTree gives s1 a subagent (sub-1) that spawned its own (sub-2), and an
// unrelated session (s9) whose edits must never show up under s1.
func seedTree(fake *webtest.Fake) {
	fake.AddSession(session.Session{ID: "sub-1", ParentSessionID: "s1", Title: "Subagent", CreatedAt: 2})
	fake.AddSession(session.Session{ID: "sub-2", ParentSessionID: "sub-1", Title: "Nested", CreatedAt: 3})
	fake.AddSession(session.Session{ID: "s9", Title: "Other"})
	for _, f := range []history.File{
		{ID: "f0", SessionID: "s1", Path: "/repo/a.go", Content: "a\n", Version: 0, CreatedAt: 10, UpdatedAt: 10},
		{ID: "f1", SessionID: "sub-1", Path: "/repo/a.go", Content: "a\nb\n", Version: 1, CreatedAt: 11, UpdatedAt: 11},
		{ID: "f2", SessionID: "s1", Path: "/repo/a.go", Content: "a\nb\nc\n", Version: 2, CreatedAt: 12, UpdatedAt: 12},
		{ID: "f3", SessionID: "sub-2", Path: "/repo/b.go", Content: "", Version: 0, CreatedAt: 13, UpdatedAt: 13},
		{ID: "f4", SessionID: "sub-2", Path: "/repo/b.go", Content: "x\n", Version: 1, CreatedAt: 14, UpdatedAt: 14},
		{ID: "f5", SessionID: "s9", Path: "/repo/c.go", Content: "c\n", Version: 0, CreatedAt: 15, UpdatedAt: 15},
	} {
		fake.AddHistory(f)
	}
	fake.SetDiagnostics(map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts{"/repo/a.go": {Error: 1, Warning: 2, Hint: 4}})
}

func diffURL(g *Gateway, sid, path, extra string) string {
	return g.URL() + "/api/sessions/" + sid + "/files/diff?path=" + url.QueryEscape(path) + extra
}

func TestFileSummariesAggregateChildSessions(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	g, fake := startTest(t)
	seedTree(fake)
	rsp, body := doJSON(t, authedClient(t, g), http.MethodGet, g.URL()+"/api/sessions/s1/files", "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rsp.StatusCode, string(body))
	var got []FileSummary
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(body, &got))
	require.Equal(t, []FileSummary{
		{Path: "/repo/b.go", Versions: 2, VersionIDs: []int64{0, 1}, Additions: 1, UpdatedAt: 14, SessionIDs: []string{"sub-2"}},
		{
			Path: "/repo/a.go", Versions: 3, VersionIDs: []int64{0, 1, 2}, Additions: 2,
			Diagnostics: FileDiagnostics{Error: 1, Warning: 2}, UpdatedAt: 12, SessionIDs: []string{"s1", "sub-1"},
		},
	}, got)
}

func TestFileDiffAcrossChildSessions(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	g, fake := startTest(t)
	seedTree(fake)
	c := authedClient(t, g)

	rsp, body := doJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, diffURL(g, "s1", "/repo/a.go", ""), "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rsp.StatusCode, string(body))
	var d FileDiff
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(body, &d))
	require.Equal(t, int64(0), d.From)
	require.Equal(t, int64(2), d.To)
	require.Equal(t, "a\n", d.Old)
	require.Equal(t, "a\nb\nc\n", d.New)
	require.Contains(t, d.Unified, "+b")
	require.Contains(t, d.Unified, "+c")
	require.Equal(t, "go", d.Language)
	require.False(t, d.Truncated)

	rsp, body = doJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, diffURL(g, "s1", "/repo/a.go", "&from=1&to=2"), "")
	require.Equal(t, http.StatusOK, rsp.StatusCode)
	require.NoError(t, json.Unmarshal(body, &d))
	require.Equal(t, "a\nb\n", d.Old, "version 1 is the subagent's edit")
}

func TestFileDiffRejectsUnknownVersion(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	g, fake := startTest(t)
	seedTree(fake)
	c := authedClient(t, g)
	for _, extra := range []string{"&from=5", "&to=abc", "&from=-1"} {
		rsp, body := doJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, diffURL(g, "s1", "/repo/a.go", extra), "")
		require.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, rsp.StatusCode, extra)
		require.Contains(t, string(body), "bad_version")
	}
}

func TestFileDiffRejectsPathOutsideTree(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	g, fake := startTest(t)
	seedTree(fake)
	c := authedClient(t, g)
	for _, p := range []string{"/repo/c.go", "../../etc/passwd", "/etc/passwd"} {
		rsp, body := doJSON(t, c, http.MethodGet, diffURL(g, "s1", p, ""), "")
		require.Equal(t, http.StatusNotFound, rsp.StatusCode, p)

	}

	require.Equal(t, http.StatusBadRequest, rsp.StatusCode)
}

func TestBuildDiffTruncatesLargePayload(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	line := func(c string) string { return strings.Repeat(c, 1023) + "\n" }
	old := strings.Repeat(line("o"), 600)
	next := strings.Repeat(line("n"), 600)
	d, err := buildDiff("/repo/big.txt", []history.File{{Version: 0, Content: old}, {Version: 1, Content: next}}, nil, nil)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.True(t, d.Truncated)
	require.Empty(t, d.Old)
	require.Empty(t, d.New)
	require.LessOrEqual(t, len(d.Unified), maxDiffBytes)
	require.True(t, strings.HasSuffix(d.Unified, "\n"), "the cut lands on a line boundary")
	require.Equal(t, "text", d.Language)
}

func TestTruncateLinesKeepsRunes(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	got := truncateLines(strings.Repeat("é", 10), 5)
	require.True(t, utf8.ValidString(got))
	require.Equal(t, "éé", got)
	require.Equal(t, "ab\n", truncateLines("ab\ncd\nef", 5))
	require.Equal(t, "short", truncateLines("short", 50))
}

func TestEncodeStripsFileContent(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	typ, raw, ok := encode(pubsub.Event[history.File]{Type: pubsub.CreatedEvent, Payload: history.File{
		ID: "f1", SessionID: "s1", Path: "/repo/a.go", Content: strings.Repeat("x", 4096), Version: 3,
	}})

	require.NotContains(t, string(raw), "xxxx")
	require.Contains(t, string(raw), `"path":"/repo/a.go"`)
	require.Contains(t, string(raw), `"version":3`)
}

}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `files.go`**

```go
package web

import (
	"cmp"
	"context"
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"path/filepath"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/diff"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/history"
)

const (
	// maxDiffBytes caps a diff answer (spec 2.7: 1 MB). Above it the old
	// and new texts are dropped and the unified diff is cut, so one
	// generated file cannot stall the tab.
	maxDiffBytes = 1 << 20
	// maxTreeSessions bounds the child-session walk. A real tree is a few
	// dozen sessions; the cap only stops a runaway or cyclic parent chain.
	maxTreeSessions = 256
)

// FileDiagnostics is the LSP diagnostic count for one edited file.
type FileDiagnostics struct {
	Error   int `json:"error"`
	Warning int `json:"warning"`
}

// FileSummary is one edited path across a session and every session below
// it.
type FileSummary struct {
	Path        string          `json:"path"`
	Versions    int             `json:"versions"`
	VersionIDs  []int64         `json:"version_ids"`
	Additions   int             `json:"additions"`
	Removals    int             `json:"removals"`
	Diagnostics FileDiagnostics `json:"diagnostics"`
	UpdatedAt   int64           `json:"updated_at"`
	SessionIDs  []string        `json:"session_ids"`
}

// FileDiff is the diff between two versions of one edited path. Truncated
// answers carry only the unified text.
type FileDiff struct {
	Path      string `json:"path"`
	From      int64  `json:"from"`
	To        int64  `json:"to"`
	Old       string `json:"old"`
	New       string `json:"new"`
	Unified   string `json:"unified"`
	Language  string `json:"language"`
	Truncated bool   `json:"truncated"`
}

var errUnknownVersion = errors.New("version is not in this file's history")

// sessionTree returns root and every session below it, breadth first.
func (g *Gateway) sessionTree(ctx context.Context, root string) ([]string, error) {
	seen := map[string]bool{root: true}
	order := []string{root}
	for i := 0; i < len(order); i++ {
		children, err := g.ws.ListChildSessions(ctx, order[i])
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		for _, c := range children {
			if seen[c.ID] || len(order) >= maxTreeSessions {
				continue
			}
			seen[c.ID] = true
			order = append(order, c.ID)
		}
	}
	return order, nil
}

// treeHistory groups the file history of root's tree by path, each path's
// versions in version order.
func (g *Gateway) treeHistory(ctx context.Context, root string) (map[string][]history.File, error) {
	ids, err := g.sessionTree(ctx, root)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	byPath := map[string][]history.File{}
	for _, id := range ids {
		files, err := g.ws.ListSessionHistory(ctx, id)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		for _, f := range files {
			byPath[f.Path] = append(byPath[f.Path], f)
		}
	}
	for _, versions := range byPath {
		slices.SortFunc(versions, func(a, b history.File) int {
			return cmp.Or(cmp.Compare(a.Version, b.Version), cmp.Compare(a.CreatedAt, b.CreatedAt))
		})
	}
	return byPath, nil
}

// fileSummaries lists root's edited files, newest first. The counts are
// first against latest, the same numbers the TUI sidebar shows.
func (g *Gateway) fileSummaries(ctx context.Context, root string) ([]FileSummary, error) {
	byPath, err := g.treeHistory(ctx, root)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	diags := g.ws.LSPDiagnosticsByFile(ctx)
	out := make([]FileSummary, 0, len(byPath))
	for path, versions := range byPath {
		first, last := versions[0], versions[len(versions)-1]
		_, additions, removals := diff.GenerateDiff(first.Content, last.Content, path)
		ids := make([]int64, len(versions))
		var sessions []string
		for i, v := range versions {
			ids[i] = v.Version
			if !slices.Contains(sessions, v.SessionID) {
				sessions = append(sessions, v.SessionID)
			}
		}
		d := diags[path]
		out = append(out, FileSummary{
			Path: path, Versions: len(versions), VersionIDs: ids,
			Additions: additions, Removals: removals,
			Diagnostics: FileDiagnostics{Error: d.Error, Warning: d.Warning},
			UpdatedAt:   last.UpdatedAt, SessionIDs: sessions,
		})
	}
	slices.SortFunc(out, func(a, b FileSummary) int {
		return cmp.Or(cmp.Compare(b.UpdatedAt, a.UpdatedAt), cmp.Compare(a.Path, b.Path))
	})
	return out, nil
}

// buildDiff diffs two versions of one path. A nil bound picks the first or
// the latest version.
func buildDiff(path string, versions []history.File, from, to *int64) (FileDiff, error) {
	pick := func(want *int64, fallback history.File) (history.File, error) {
		if want == nil {
			return fallback, nil
		}
		for _, v := range versions {
			if v.Version == *want {
				return v, nil
			}
		}
		return history.File{}, errUnknownVersion
	}
	a, err := pick(from, versions[0])
	if err != nil {
		return FileDiff{}, err
	}
	b, err := pick(to, versions[len(versions)-1])
	if err != nil {
		return FileDiff{}, err
	}
	unified, _, _ := diff.GenerateDiff(a.Content, b.Content, path)
	d := FileDiff{
		Path: path, From: a.Version, To: b.Version,
		Old: a.Content, New: b.Content, Unified: unified, Language: languageFor(path),
	}
	if len(d.Old)+len(d.New)+len(d.Unified) > maxDiffBytes {
		d.Old, d.New, d.Truncated = "", "", true
		d.Unified = truncateLines(d.Unified, maxDiffBytes)
	}
	return d, nil
}

// truncateLines cuts s to at most limit bytes, at the last line break when
// there is one and never inside a UTF-8 sequence.
func truncateLines(s string, limit int) string {
	if len(s) <= limit {
		return s
	}
	if i := strings.LastIndexByte(s[:limit], '\n'); i >= 0 {
		return s[:i+1]
	}
	cut := limit
	for cut > 0 && !utf8.RuneStart(s[cut]) {
		cut--
	}
	return s[:cut]
}

var languages = map[string]string{
	".go": "go", ".ts": "typescript", ".mts": "typescript", ".cts": "typescript", ".tsx": "tsx",
	".js": "javascript", ".mjs": "javascript", ".cjs": "javascript", ".jsx": "jsx",
	".json": "json", ".css": "css", ".html": "html", ".htm": "html",
	".md": "markdown", ".py": "python", ".yaml": "yaml", ".yml": "yaml",
}

// languageFor names the syntax the diff view highlights; the browser maps
// each name to a lazily loaded CodeMirror language.
func languageFor(path string) string {
	if l, ok := languages[strings.ToLower(filepath.Ext(path))]; ok {
		return l
	}
	return "text"
}

func (g *Gateway) handleFiles(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	files, err := g.fileSummaries(r.Context(), r.PathValue("sid"))
	if err != nil {
		writeInternal(w, "list_files", err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, files)
}

// handleFileDiff answers only for paths in the session tree's history, and
// reads nothing but that history: a path is a lookup key, never a file to
// open.
func (g *Gateway) handleFileDiff(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	q := r.URL.Query()
	path := q.Get("path")
	if path == "" {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "missing_path", "path is required")
		return
	}
	from, okFrom := versionParam(q.Get("from"))
	to, okTo := versionParam(q.Get("to"))
	if !okFrom || !okTo {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_version", "from and to must be version numbers")
		return
	}
	byPath, err := g.treeHistory(r.Context(), r.PathValue("sid"))
	if err != nil {
		writeInternal(w, "file_history", err)
		return
	}
	versions, ok := byPath[path]
	if !ok {
		writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "unknown_path", "the session has no history for this path")
		return
	}
	d, err := buildDiff(path, versions, from, to)
	if errors.Is(err, errUnknownVersion) {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "bad_version", err.Error())
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, d)
}

func versionParam(s string) (*int64, bool) {
	if s == "" {
		return nil, true
	}
	n, err := strconv.ParseInt(s, 10, 64)
	if err != nil || n < 0 {
		return nil, false
	}
	return &n, true
}
```

Register in `registerAPI`:

```go
	api.HandleFunc("GET /api/sessions/{sid}/files", g.handleFiles)
	api.HandleFunc("GET /api/sessions/{sid}/files/diff", g.handleFileDiff)
```

In `encode`, next to the message case:

```go
	if p.Type == pubsub.PayloadTypeFile {
		return p.Type, withoutFileContent(p.Payload), true
	}
```

```go
// withoutFileContent drops the file text from a history event. The tab
// only needs to know that a path changed, and whole files in the replay
// ring would cost memory for every reconnecting tab.
func withoutFileContent(payload json.RawMessage) json.RawMessage {
	var env map[string]json.RawMessage
	if err := json.Unmarshal(payload, &env); err != nil {
		return payload
	}
	var file map[string]json.RawMessage
	if err := json.Unmarshal(env["payload"], &file); err != nil {
		return payload
	}
	delete(file, "content")
	inner, err := json.Marshal(file)
	if err != nil {
		return payload
	}
	env["payload"] = inner
	out, err := json.Marshal(env)
	if err != nil {
		return payload
	}
	return out
}
```

```go
func writeTeam(t *testing.T, dir string, state agentteam.State) {

	data, err := json.Marshal(state)
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "team.json"), data, 0o644))
}

func writeRuns(t *testing.T, dir string, runs []agentrun.Run) {

	data, err := json.Marshal(map[string]any{"version": 1, "runs": runs})
	require.NoError(t, err)
	require.NoError(t, os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(dir, "agents.json"), data, 0o644))

func TestTeamWatcherEmitsOnlyOnChange(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	dir := t.TempDir()
	writeTeam(t, dir, agentteam.State{TeamID: "team-1", Members: []agentteam.Member{alice()}})
	w := newTeamWatcher(dir)

	team, runs := w.poll()
	require.NotNil(t, team)
	require.Contains(t, string(team), `"name":"alice"`)
	require.JSONEq(t, `[]`, string(runs))

	require.Nil(t, team, "an unchanged file publishes nothing")
	require.Nil(t, runs)

}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `team.go`**

```go
package web

import (
	"bytes"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"log/slog"
	"net/http"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"time"

	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/agent"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/agentrun"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/agentteam"
)

// teamPollInterval matches the TUI sidebar's refresh (teamRefreshInterval).
const teamPollInterval = time.Second

// TeamTask is one shared task as the task board shows it.
type TeamTask struct {
	ID          string               `json:"id"`
	Revision    int                  `json:"revision"`
	Subject     string               `json:"subject"`
	Description string               `json:"description"`
	Status      agentteam.TaskStatus `json:"status"`
	OwnerID     string               `json:"owner_id,omitempty"`
	OwnerName   string               `json:"owner_name,omitempty"`
	BlockedBy   []string             `json:"blocked_by"`
	Ready       bool                 `json:"ready"`
	SessionID   string               `json:"session_id,omitempty"`
}

// TeamState is the workspace's Agent Team roster and live task board. The
// inbox stays out: it can hold large messages the browser never shows.
type TeamState struct {
	TeamID  string             `json:"team_id"`
	Members []agentteam.Member `json:"members"`
	Tasks   []TeamTask         `json:"tasks"`
}

func emptyTeam() TeamState {
	return TeamState{Members: []agentteam.Member{}, Tasks: []TeamTask{}}
}

func teamStateFrom(s agentteam.State) TeamState {
	out := emptyTeam()
	out.TeamID = s.TeamID
	if s.Members != nil {
		out.Members = s.Members
	}
	for _, t := range agentteam.TaskViews(s) {
		if t.Status == agentteam.TaskDeleted {
			continue
		}
		blocked := t.BlockedBy
		if blocked == nil {
			blocked = []string{}
		}
		out.Tasks = append(out.Tasks, TeamTask{
			ID: t.ID, Revision: t.Revision, Subject: t.Subject, Description: t.Description,
			Status: t.Status, OwnerID: t.OwnerID, OwnerName: t.OwnerName,
			BlockedBy: blocked, Ready: t.Ready, SessionID: t.SessionID,
		})
	}
	return out
}

// teamWatcher rereads team.json and agents.json and reports what changed.
type teamWatcher struct {
	statePath, runsPath string

	mu      sync.Mutex
	team    TeamState
	runs    []agentrun.Run
	teamRaw []byte
	runsRaw []byte
	warned  bool
}

func newTeamWatcher(dir string) *teamWatcher {
	w := &teamWatcher{team: emptyTeam(), runs: []agentrun.Run{}}
	if dir != "" {
		w.statePath = filepath.Join(dir, "team.json")
		w.runsPath = filepath.Join(dir, "agents.json")
	}
	return w
}

// poll returns the payloads that changed since the last poll, nil for an
// unchanged one. A team file that fails to load keeps the last good roster:
// the writer replaces it atomically, so a failure is a real problem, and
// blanking the board would only make it flicker.
func (w *teamWatcher) poll() (team, runs json.RawMessage) {
	if w.statePath == "" {
		return nil, nil
	}
	w.mu.Lock()
	defer w.mu.Unlock()
	if state, err := agentteam.LoadState(w.statePath); err != nil {
		if !w.warned {
			slog.Warn("Web mode could not read the team state", "error", err)
			w.warned = true
		}
	} else {
		w.warned = false
		next := teamStateFrom(state)
		if raw, err := json.Marshal(next); err == nil && !bytes.Equal(raw, w.teamRaw) {
			w.team, w.teamRaw, team = next, raw, raw
		}
	}
	list := agentrun.Load(w.runsPath)
	if list == nil {
		list = []agentrun.Run{}
	}
	if raw, err := json.Marshal(list); err == nil && !bytes.Equal(raw, w.runsRaw) {
		w.runs, w.runsRaw, runs = list, raw, raw
	}
	return team, runs
}

// current returns the last good team state and runs for a snapshot.
func (w *teamWatcher) current() (TeamState, []agentrun.Run) {
	w.mu.Lock()
	defer w.mu.Unlock()
	return w.team, w.runs
}

func (g *Gateway) watchTeam(interval time.Duration) {
	t := time.NewTicker(interval)
	defer t.Stop()
	for {
		select {
		case <-g.ctx.Done():
			return
		case <-t.C:
			team, runs := g.team.poll()
			if team != nil {
				g.hub.publish(frameTeamState, team)
			}
			if runs != nil {
				g.hub.publish(frameAgentRuns, runs)
			}
		}
	}
}

func (g *Gateway) handleGetSession(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	s, err := g.ws.GetSession(r.Context(), r.PathValue("sid"))
	if err != nil {
		if isNotFound(err) {
			writeError(w, http.StatusNotFound, "unknown_session", "no such session")
			return
		}
		writeInternal(w, "get_session", err)
		return
	}
	writeJSON(w, http.StatusOK, innerPayload(pubsub.Event[session.Session]{Type: pubsub.UpdatedEvent, Payload: s}))
}

func (g *Gateway) handleTeamMessage(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var body struct {
		SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
		Text      string `json:"text"`
	}
	if !decodeJSON(w, r, &body) {
		return
	}
	switch {
	case body.SessionID == "":
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "missing_session", "session_id is required")
		return
	case strings.TrimSpace(body.Text) == "":
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "empty_message", "message is empty")
		return
	case len(body.Text) > agentteam.DefaultMaxMessageBytes:
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "message_too_large", "message is over 64 KiB")
		return
	}
	if err := g.ws.TeamSendMessage(r.Context(), body.SessionID, r.PathValue("member"), body.Text); err != nil {
		writeTeamError(w, err)
		return
	}
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func (g *Gateway) handleTeamInterrupt(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	var body struct {
		SessionID string `json:"session_id"`
	}
	if !decodeJSON(w, r, &body) {
		return
	}
	if body.SessionID == "" {
		writeError(w, http.StatusBadRequest, "missing_session", "session_id is required")
		return
	}
	if err := g.ws.TeamInterrupt(r.Context(), body.SessionID, r.PathValue("member")); err != nil {
		writeTeamError(w, err)
		return
	}
	w.WriteHeader(http.StatusNoContent)
}

func writeTeamError(w http.ResponseWriter, err error) {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, agent.ErrAgentTeamsDisabled):
		writeError(w, http.StatusConflict, "teams_disabled", "Agent Teams is not enabled")
	case agentteam.ErrorCode(err) != "":
		// Team errors are written for the model and name only the member
		// and the rule it broke, so they are safe to show.
		writeError(w, http.StatusConflict, "team_refused", err.Error())
	default:
		writeInternal(w, "team_failed", err)
	}
}
```

```go
func teamDir(opts Options) string {
	if opts.AgentTeamsDir != "" {
		return opts.AgentTeamsDir
	}
	cfg := opts.Workspace.Config()
	if cfg == nil || cfg.Options == nil || cfg.Options.DataDirectory == "" {
		return ""
	}
	return filepath.Join(cfg.Options.DataDirectory, "agent-teams")
}
```

```go
func fixtureRuns() []agentrun.Run {

	return []agentrun.Run{

// gatewayFixtureFrames builds the frames the gateway originates itself,
// which never pass through encode.
func gatewayFixtureFrames(t *testing.T) map[string]frame {
	t.Helper()
	mk := func(typ string, v any) frame {
		raw, err := json.Marshal(v)
		require.NoError(t, err)
		return frame{Seq: 1, Type: typ, Payload: raw}
	}
	return map[string]frame{
		"team_state": mk(frameTeamState, fixtureTeam()),
		"agent_runs": mk(frameAgentRuns, fixtureRuns()),
	}
}
```

```go
import (

func TestScriptedPlaysAReply(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	f := NewFake()
	events := f.SubscribeEvents(t.Context())
	require.NoError(t, Scripted(f)(t.Context(), "s1", "hello", nil))
	var last message.Message
	var sess session.Session
	for len(events) > 0 {
		switch e := (<-events).(type) {
		case pubsub.Event[message.Message]:
			last = e.Payload
		case pubsub.Event[session.Session]:
			sess = e.Payload
		}
	}
	require.Contains(t, last.Content().Text, "Signal received. hello")
	require.EqualValues(t, 1200, sess.PromptTokens)
}

func TestScriptedIgnoresStaleDecision(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	f := NewFake()
	// A grant left over from an earlier run.
	f.PermissionGrant(permission.PermissionRequest{ID: "old"})
	done := make(chan error, 1)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(t.Context())
	defer cancel()
	go func() { done <- Scripted(f)(ctx, "s1", "run permission", nil) }()
	select {
	case <-done:
		t.Fatal("a stale grant resolved the new request")
	case <-time.After(400 * time.Millisecond):
	}
	f.PermissionGrant(permission.PermissionRequest{ID: "new"})
	require.NoError(t, <-done)
}

func TestSeedHolodeckWritesTeamFiles(t *testing.T) {
	t.Parallel()
	f := NewFake()
	dir := t.TempDir()
	require.NoError(t, SeedHolodeck(f, dir, time.Date(2026, 11, 22, 12, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)))

	team, err := os.ReadFile(filepath.Join(dir, "team.json"))

}
```

```go
// drainResolutions forgets answers left over from an earlier run, so a
// stale grant cannot resolve this run's request.
func (f *Fake) drainResolutions() {
	for {
		select {
		case <-f.decisions:
		case <-f.answers:
		default:
			return
		}
	}
}
```

`seed.go`:

```go
package webtest

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"time"

	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/agentrun"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/agentteam"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/history"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/lsp"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/message"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/pubsub"
	"github.com/charmbracelet/crush/internal/session"
)

const parserV2 = "package parser\n\n// Parse reads one token stream.\nfunc Parse(src string) []Token {\n\treturn tokenize(src)\n}\n"

func (f *Fake) parserPath() string { return filepath.Join(f.WorkingDir(), "internal", "parser", "parser.go") }

// SeedHolodeck gives the fake a teammate, a subagent, edited files and
// todos, so every M2 surface has something to show. teamDir receives
// team.json and agents.json for Options.AgentTeamsDir. Messages are dated
// from clock so a pinned browser clock shows stable elapsed times; the
// registry runs use the wall clock because agentrun.Load prunes by it.
func SeedHolodeck(f *Fake, teamDir string, clock time.Time) error {
	ts := clock.Add(-42 * time.Second).Unix()
	app := filepath.Join(f.WorkingDir(), "web", "src", "app.ts")

	root, _ := f.GetSession(context.Background(), "s1")
	root.Todos = []session.Todo{
		{Content: "Map the parser", Status: session.TodoStatusCompleted, ActiveForm: "Mapping the parser"},
		{Content: "Port the parser", Status: session.TodoStatusInProgress, ActiveForm: "Porting the parser"},
		{Content: "Write parser tests", Status: session.TodoStatusPending, ActiveForm: "Writing parser tests"},
	}
	f.AddSession(root)
	f.AddSession(session.Session{ID: "sub-1", ParentSessionID: "s1", Title: "Map the parser", CreatedAt: ts, PromptTokens: 3100, CompletionTokens: 220, Cost: 0.004})
	f.AddSession(session.Session{ID: "team-m-1", ParentSessionID: "s1", Title: "Teammate alice", CreatedAt: ts, PromptTokens: 8200, CompletionTokens: 640, Cost: 0.031})

	f.AddMessage(message.Message{ID: "sub-a-1", SessionID: "sub-1", Role: message.Assistant, CreatedAt: ts, UpdatedAt: ts, Model: "claude-haiku", Provider: "anthropic", Parts: []message.ContentPart{
		message.TextContent{Text: "Scanning the parser package for the tokenizer entry points."},
		message.ToolCall{ID: "sub-tc-1", Name: "grep", Input: `{"pattern":"func Parse"}`},
	}})
	f.AddMessage(message.Message{ID: "alice-a-1", SessionID: "team-m-1", Role: message.Assistant, CreatedAt: ts, UpdatedAt: ts, Model: "claude-sonnet", Provider: "anthropic", Parts: []message.ContentPart{
		message.TextContent{Text: "Porting the parser to the new token stream."},
		message.ToolCall{ID: "alice-tc-1", Name: "edit", Input: `{"file_path":"internal/parser/parser.go"}`, Finished: true},
		message.Finish{Reason: message.FinishReasonToolUse, Time: ts},
	}})
	f.AddMessage(message.Message{ID: "alice-t-1", SessionID: "team-m-1", Role: message.Tool, CreatedAt: ts, UpdatedAt: ts, Parts: []message.ContentPart{
		message.ToolResult{ToolCallID: "alice-tc-1", Name: "edit", Content: "Edited internal/parser/parser.go"},
	}})

	for _, h := range []history.File{
		{ID: "h-0", SessionID: "s1", Path: f.parserPath(), Content: "package parser\n\nfunc Parse() {}\n", Version: 0, CreatedAt: ts, UpdatedAt: ts},
		{ID: "h-1", SessionID: "s1", Path: f.parserPath(), Content: "package parser\n\nfunc Parse(src string) {}\n", Version: 1, CreatedAt: ts + 1, UpdatedAt: ts + 1},
		{ID: "h-2", SessionID: "team-m-1", Path: f.parserPath(), Content: parserV2, Version: 2, CreatedAt: ts + 2, UpdatedAt: ts + 2},
		{ID: "h-3", SessionID: "team-m-1", Path: app, Content: "", Version: 0, CreatedAt: ts + 3, UpdatedAt: ts + 3},
		{ID: "h-4", SessionID: "team-m-1", Path: app, Content: "export const app = mount();\n", Version: 1, CreatedAt: ts + 4, UpdatedAt: ts + 4},
	} {
		f.AddHistory(h)
	}
	f.SetDiagnostics(map[string]lsp.DiagnosticCounts{f.parserPath(): {Error: 1}})

	team := agentteam.State{
		Version: 1, TeamID: "team-holodeck", CreatedAt: clock,
		Members: []agentteam.Member{
			{ID: "lead", Name: agentteam.LeadName, Role: agentteam.RoleLead, SessionID: "s1"},
			{ID: "m-1", Name: "alice", Role: agentteam.RoleTeammate, Description: "Ports the parser", SessionID: "team-m-1", ParentID: "s1", Status: agentteam.StatusRunning, Model: "claude-sonnet", ModelProvider: "anthropic"},
		},
		Tasks: []agentteam.Task{
			{ID: "task-1", Revision: 2, Subject: "Port the parser", Description: "Move Parse onto the token stream.", Status: agentteam.TaskInProgress, OwnerID: "m-1", BlockedBy: []string{}, WriteScopes: []string{"internal/parser"}, SessionID: "s1"},
			{ID: "task-2", Revision: 1, Subject: "Write parser tests", Description: "Cover the new token stream.", Status: agentteam.TaskPending, BlockedBy: []string{"task-1"}, WriteScopes: []string{}, SessionID: "s1"},
			{ID: "task-3", Revision: 3, Subject: "Map the parser", Description: "List the entry points.", Status: agentteam.TaskCompleted, BlockedBy: []string{}, WriteScopes: []string{}, SessionID: "s1"},
		},
	}
	now := time.Now()
	runs := map[string]any{"version": 1, "runs": []agentrun.Run{
		{ID: "sub-1", Kind: agentrun.KindSubagent, Name: "Map the parser", SessionID: "sub-1", ParentSessionID: "s1", Status: agentrun.StatusRunning, StartedAt: now},
		{ID: "team-m-1", Kind: agentrun.KindTeammate, Name: "alice", SessionID: "team-m-1", ParentSessionID: "s1", Status: agentrun.StatusRunning, StartedAt: now},
	}}
	if err := os.MkdirAll(teamDir, 0o755); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	for name, v := range map[string]any{"team.json": team, "agents.json": runs} {
		data, err := json.Marshal(v)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(teamDir, name), data, 0o644); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// delegate plays the subagent picking up work: a new edit call on sub-1
// and version 3 of parser.go.
func (f *Fake) delegate() {
	now := time.Now().Unix()
	call := message.ToolCall{ID: f.nextID("sub-tc"), Name: "edit", Input: `{"file_path":"internal/parser/parser.go"}`}
	m := message.Message{ID: f.nextID("sub-a"), SessionID: "sub-1", Role: message.Assistant, CreatedAt: now, UpdatedAt: now, Parts: []message.ContentPart{
		message.TextContent{Text: "Rewriting the tokenizer loop."}, call,
	}}
	f.AddMessage(m)
	f.Emit(pubsub.Event[message.Message]{Type: pubsub.CreatedEvent, Payload: m})
	h := history.File{ID: f.nextID("h"), SessionID: "sub-1", Path: f.parserPath(), Content: parserV2 + "\nfunc tokenize(string) []Token { return nil }\n", Version: 3, CreatedAt: now, UpdatedAt: now}
	f.AddHistory(h)
	f.Emit(pubsub.Event[history.File]{Type: pubsub.CreatedEvent, Payload: h})
}
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { stderrBuffer } from './stderr';

test('redacts a token split across chunks', () => {
  const b = stderrBuffer(64);
  b.push('Dev server: open http://localhost:5173/#t=abc');
  b.push('def123\nnext line\n');
  expect(b.text()).not.toContain('def123');
  expect(b.text()).toContain('#t=<redacted>');
});

test('keeps only the tail and never a token fragment', () => {
  const b = stderrBuffer(32);
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) b.push(`line ${i} #t=SECRET${i}\n`);
  expect(b.text()).not.toMatch(/SECRET/);
  expect(b.text().length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(32);
});

test('redacts a partial last line', () => {
  const b = stderrBuffer(64);
  b.push('boom #t=HALF');
  expect(b.text()).toBe('boom #t=<redacted>');
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`web/e2e/stderr.ts`:

```ts
// The launch line carries the session token. Only whole lines are kept,
// redacted as they complete, so a token split across two chunks or cut by
// the size cap never reaches the buffer in part.
export function redact(text: string): string {
  return text.replace(/#t=\S+/g, '#t=<redacted>');
}

export function stderrBuffer(limit = 4096) {
  let kept = '';
  let pending = '';
  return {
    push(chunk: string) {
      pending += chunk;
      const end = pending.lastIndexOf('\n');
      if (end >= 0) {
        kept = (kept + redact(pending.slice(0, end + 1))).slice(-limit);
        pending = pending.slice(end + 1);
      }
      // A line that never ends is dropped whole rather than cut, since a
      // cut could keep the tail of a token without its #t= marker.
      if (pending.length > limit) pending = '';
    },
    text(): string {
      return (kept + redact(pending)).trim().slice(-limit);
    },
  };
}
```

In `fixture.ts`, import `{ redact, stderrBuffer }` from `./stderr`, delete
the local `redact`, and replace the `stderrText` accumulation with:

```ts
    proc.stderr!.on('data', (chunk: Buffer) => stderr.push(chunk.toString()));
    const launch = await waitForLaunch(proc, () => stderr.text());
```

(`waitForLaunch` already calls `redact` on what it is given; it stays, since

```ts
    id: 'a', role: 'assistant', session_id: 'sub-1', model: '', provider: '', created_at: 100, updated_at: 101, parts: [], ...over,

    const result = msg({ id: 't', role: 'tool', parts: [{ type: 'tool_result', data: { tool_call_id: 'c1', name: 'grep', content: '', metadata: '', is_error: false } }] });
    expect(currentTool([call, result])).toBeNull();
    const ended = msg({ parts: [{ type: 'finish', data: { reason: 'end_turn', time: 1 } }] });
    expect(currentTool([ended])).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
  session: (sid: string) => call<Session>('GET', `/api/sessions/${encodeURIComponent(sid)}`),
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Reducer**

Add the fields to `State` and `initialState()` (`files: []`, `filesStale: false`,
`team: emptyTeam()`, `runs: []`, `activity: {}`, `inspected: null`), and:

```ts
export interface AgentCall { callId: string; tool: string }
export interface Inspected { sessionId: string; session: Session | null; messages: Message[]; loading: boolean }

export function emptyTeam(): TeamState {
  return { team_id: '', members: [], tasks: [] };
}

// inspect selects the agent the inspector shows. The main session's
// transcript is already in the state, so only a child session loads.
export function inspect(s: State, sid: string | null): State {
  if (!sid) return s.inspected ? { ...s, inspected: null } : s;
  if (s.inspected?.sessionId === sid) return s;
  return { ...s, inspected: { sessionId: sid, session: null, messages: [], loading: sid !== s.sessionId } };
}

// mergeMessages lays the frames that arrived while a transcript loaded over
// the fetched copy: for one id the newer update wins, and a tie goes to the
// live frame.
export function mergeMessages(fetched: Message[], live: Message[]): Message[] {
  let out = fetched;
  for (const m of live) {
    const old = out.find((x) => x.id === m.id);
    if (!old || old.updated_at <= m.updated_at) out = upsertMessage(out, m);
  }
  return out;
}

export function mergeInspected(s: State, sid: string, fetched: Message[], session: Session | null): State {
  const ins = s.inspected;
  if (!ins || ins.sessionId !== sid) return s;
  return { ...s, inspected: { sessionId: sid, session: ins.session ?? session, messages: mergeMessages(fetched, ins.messages), loading: false } };
}

function lastToolCall(m: Message): { id: string; name: string } | null {
  for (let i = m.parts.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const p = m.parts[i];
    if (p.type === 'tool_call') return p.data;
  }
  return null;
}
```

`adoptSession` also resets `files: [], filesStale: false, activity: {}, inspected: null`.
`applySnapshot` adds `files: snap.files ?? []`, `filesStale: false`,
`team: snap.team ?? emptyTeam()`, `runs: snap.agent_runs ?? []` and keeps
`inspected` and `activity` (the snapshot carries neither).

In `reduce`, the `message` case becomes:

```ts

      const m = ev.payload as Message;
      let base: State = next;

        base = { ...base, inspected: { ...ins, messages } };

      const messages = upsertMessage(s.messages, m);
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Roster** (`web/src/state/agents.ts`)

```ts
import type { AgentRun, Message, TeamMember, TeamState, TeamTask } from '../api/types';
import { needsAttention, type State } from './reducer';

export type AgentKind = 'main' | 'teammate' | 'subagent';
export type AgentStatus = 'running' | 'inactive' | 'provisioning' | 'failed' | 'done';

export interface Agent {
  sessionId: string;
  parentSessionId: string;
  name: string;
  kind: AgentKind;
  status: AgentStatus;
  model: string;
  modelProvider: string;
  startedAt: number;
  finishedAt: number;
  member?: string;
}

const LABELS: Record<AgentStatus, string> = {
  running: 'Running', inactive: 'Inactive', provisioning: 'Starting', failed: 'Failed', done: 'Done',
};

export function statusLabel(status: AgentStatus): string {
  return LABELS[status];
}

// sessionTree returns root plus every session reachable through parent to
// child links, like stateSessionTree in internal/ui/model/team.go.
export function sessionTree(root: string, links: Array<[string, string]>): Set<string> {
  const tree = new Set<string>();
  if (!root) return tree;
  tree.add(root);
  for (let changed = true; changed; ) {
    changed = false;
    for (const [parent, child] of links) {
      if (tree.has(parent) && !tree.has(child)) {
        tree.add(child);
        changed = true;
      }
    }
  }
  return tree;
}

const retained = (id: string) => id !== '' && !id.startsWith('title-') && !id.startsWith('helper-');
const time = (iso?: string) => (iso ? Date.parse(iso) || 0 : 0);

function memberStatus(m: TeamMember): AgentStatus {
  if (m.failed || m.status === 'failed') return 'failed';
  return m.status ?? 'inactive';
}

// freshest keeps one registry record per child session: the latest start,
// running winning a tie, as freshestAgentRuns does in the TUI.
function freshest(runs: AgentRun[]): AgentRun[] {
  const best = new Map<string, AgentRun>();
  for (const r of runs) {
    const key = r.session_id ?? '';
    const cur = best.get(key);
    const newer = !cur || time(r.started_at) > time(cur.started_at)
      || (time(r.started_at) === time(cur.started_at) && r.status === 'running' && cur.status !== 'running');
    if (newer) best.set(key, r);
  }
  return [...best.values()];
}

function byLiveness(a: Agent, b: Agent): number {
  const ar = a.status === 'running' || a.status === 'provisioning';
  const br = b.status === 'running' || b.status === 'provisioning';
  if (ar !== br) return ar ? -1 : 1;
  const c = ar ? a.startedAt - b.startedAt : b.finishedAt - a.finishedAt;
  return c || a.sessionId.localeCompare(b.sessionId);
}

export function deriveAgents(root: string, team: TeamState, runs: AgentRun[]): Agent[] {
  if (!root) return [];
  const links: Array<[string, string]> = [];
  for (const m of team.members) if (m.parent_session_id && m.session_id) links.push([m.parent_session_id, m.session_id]);
  for (const r of runs) if (r.parent_session_id && r.session_id) links.push([r.parent_session_id, r.session_id]);
  const tree = sessionTree(root, links);
  const out = new Map<string, Agent>();
  for (const m of team.members) {
    if (m.role === 'lead' || !m.session_id || !m.parent_session_id || !tree.has(m.parent_session_id)) continue;
    out.set(m.session_id, {
      sessionId: m.session_id, parentSessionId: m.parent_session_id, name: m.name, kind: 'teammate',
      status: memberStatus(m), model: m.model ?? '', modelProvider: m.model_provider ?? '',
      startedAt: 0, finishedAt: 0, member: m.name,
    });
  }
  for (const r of freshest(runs)) {
    if (!r.session_id || !r.parent_session_id || !tree.has(r.parent_session_id)) continue;
    const startedAt = time(r.started_at);
    const finishedAt = time(r.finished_at);
    const known = out.get(r.session_id);
    // A teammate's roster entry owns its status: a finished turn leaves it
    // inactive, not done, because it can be woken again.
    if (known) {
      out.set(r.session_id, { ...known, startedAt, finishedAt });
      continue;
    }
    out.set(r.session_id, {
      sessionId: r.session_id, parentSessionId: r.parent_session_id, name: r.name, kind: r.kind,
      status: r.status, model: '', modelProvider: '', startedAt, finishedAt,
      member: r.kind === 'teammate' ? r.name : undefined,
    });
  }
  return [...out.values()].filter((a) => retained(a.sessionId)).sort(byLiveness);
}

export function mainAgent(s: State): Agent {
  return {
    sessionId: s.sessionId, parentSessionId: '', name: 'Coder', kind: 'main',
    status: s.busy || needsAttention(s) ? 'running' : 'inactive',
    model: s.model.name || s.model.id, modelProvider: s.model.provider, startedAt: 0, finishedAt: 0,
  };
}

export function agentsOf(s: State): Agent[] {
  return [mainAgent(s), ...deriveAgents(s.sessionId, s.team, s.runs)];
}

export function findAgent(s: State, sid: string): Agent | null {
  return agentsOf(s).find((a) => a.sessionId === sid) ?? null;
}

// currentTool is the newest call of the newest assistant turn that has no
// result yet; `since` is when that turn last changed, in milliseconds.
export function currentTool(messages: Message[]): { name: string; since: number } | null {
  const done = new Set<string>();
  for (const m of messages) for (const p of m.parts) if (p.type === 'tool_result') done.add(p.data.tool_call_id);
  for (let i = messages.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    const m = messages[i];
    if (m.role !== 'assistant') continue;
    const finish = m.parts.find((p) => p.type === 'finish');
    if (finish && finish.type === 'finish' && finish.data.reason !== 'tool_use') return null;
    for (let j = m.parts.length - 1; j >= 0; j--) {
      const p = m.parts[j];
      if (p.type === 'tool_call' && !done.has(p.data.id)) return { name: p.data.name, since: m.updated_at * 1000 };
    }
    return null;
  }
  return null;
}

export function scopedTasks(root: string, team: TeamState): TeamTask[] {
  const links: Array<[string, string]> = [];
  for (const m of team.members) if (m.parent_session_id && m.session_id) links.push([m.parent_session_id, m.session_id]);
  const tree = sessionTree(root, links);
  return team.tasks.filter((t) => !!t.session_id && tree.has(t.session_id));
}

export function agentTask(team: TeamState, agent: Agent): TeamTask | null {
  const member = team.members.find((m) => m.session_id === agent.sessionId);
  if (!member) return null;
  const owned = team.tasks.filter((t) => t.owner_id === member.id);
  return owned.find((t) => t.status === 'in_progress') ?? owned.find((t) => t.status === 'pending') ?? null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Store**

```ts
const FILES_DEBOUNCE_MS = 400;
let filesTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

async function refreshFiles(sid: string) {
  try {
    const files = await api.files(sid);
    dispatch((s) => (s.sessionId === sid ? { ...s, files, filesStale: false } : s));
  } catch {
    // The list stays as it is; the next file frame or snapshot repairs it.
    dispatch((s) => ({ ...s, filesStale: false }));
  }
}

// scheduleFiles coalesces a burst of file frames (a multiedit writes one
// version per edit) into one list request.
function scheduleFiles() {
  if (filesTimer !== null) return;
  filesTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    filesTimer = null;
    const sid = state.value.sessionId;
    if (sid) void refreshFiles(sid);
  }, FILES_DEBOUNCE_MS);
}

async function loadInspected(sid: string) {
  try {
    const [messages, session] = await Promise.all([api.messages(sid), api.session(sid).catch(() => null)]);
    dispatch((s) => mergeInspected(s, sid, messages, session));
  } catch (err) {
    dispatch((s) => (s.inspected?.sessionId === sid
      ? pushToast({ ...s, inspected: { ...s.inspected, loading: false } }, 'error', `Could not load the agent: ${errorText(err)}`)
      : s));
  }
}
```

At the end of `handleFrames`:

```ts
  // A snapshot means frames may have been missed, so the inspected child
  // transcript is fetched again.
```

```ts
      return null;

    if (!sid) return 'There is no session to interrupt from';
    try {

      return null;
    } catch (err) {
      return errorText(err);
    }
  },
  // cancelAgent stops a subagent through its own session, which is how the
  // coordinator reaches a dispatched subagent's run.
  async cancelAgent(sessionId: string): Promise<string | null> {
    try {
      await api.cancel(sessionId);
      return null;
    } catch (err) {
      return errorText(err);
    }
```

`storage.ts`:

```ts
// Storage can throw: a blocked third-party context, Safari private mode or
// a full quota. A preference that cannot be read falls back to its default
// and one that cannot be written lasts for this tab.
export function readStored(key: string): string | null {
  try {
    return localStorage.getItem(key);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function writeStored(key: string, value: string): void {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(key, value);
  } catch {
    // Kept in memory only; see above.
  }
}
```

`motion.ts` uses `readStored`/`writeStored` instead of `localStorage`.
