### Task 12: Shared transcript and log hardening

The inspector shows an agent's transcript with the same cards as the
transmission log, so the log body becomes a shared `Transcript`. Deferred
G1-35: remote markdown images, `MessageView` re-rendering for every state
change, untruncated tool input and shell output, an uncapped "Show full
output", no re-pin when the user sends, and missing component tests.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/ui/log/Transcript.tsx`, `web/src/ui/log/Transcript.test.tsx`, `web/src/ui/log/Clipped.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/ui/log/TransmissionLog.tsx`, `MessageView.tsx`, `ToolCard.tsx`, `Markdown.tsx`
- Test: `web/src/ui/log/TransmissionLog.test.tsx`, `web/src/ui/log/Markdown.test.tsx`, `web/src/ui/log/MessageView.test.tsx` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `Transcript({ messages, resetKey, label, empty }: { messages: Message[]; resetKey: string; label: string; empty?: ComponentChildren })`: a `role="log"` scroller, windowed to 200 messages with "Show earlier", pinned to the bottom until the user scrolls up, re-pinned when a new user message arrives.
  - `MessageView` keeps its props and skips re-rendering when its message and that message's tool results are unchanged.
  - `Clipped({ text, label })`: shows `PREVIEW = 4000` characters, then "Show full {label} (N KB)" up to `FULL_CAP = 256 * 1024` characters, then a note.
  - Remote images (anything but `data:` and `blob:`) render as links.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`Clipped.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useState } from 'preact/hooks';

export const PREVIEW = 4000;
// Even "show full" stops here: a multi-megabyte output in one <pre> stalls
// layout for every later frame.
export const FULL_CAP = 256 * 1024;

export function Clipped({ text, label }: { text: string; label: string }) {
  const [full, setFull] = useState(false);
  const limit = full ? FULL_CAP : PREVIEW;
  const shown = text.length <= limit ? text : text.slice(0, limit);
  return (
    <>
      <pre>{shown}</pre>
      {!full && text.length > PREVIEW && (
        <button type="button" class="link-button" onClick={() => setFull(true)}>
          Show full {label} ({Math.round(text.length / 1024)} KB)
        </button>
      )}
      {full && text.length > FULL_CAP && (
        <p class="hud-label">Showing the first 256 KB; the terminal has the rest.</p>
      )}
    </>
  );
}
```

`ToolCard.tsx` renders `<Clipped text={pretty(call.input)} label="input" />`
for the input and `<Clipped text={content} label="output" />` for the
output (drop its own `full` state and `PREVIEW`). `MessageView`'s
`shell_command` case uses `<Clipped text={p.data.output} label="output" />`.

`MessageView.tsx`: rename the function to `MessageViewBody`, export:

```tsx
import { Component } from 'preact';

type Props = { m: Message; results: Map<string, ToolResultData> };

// The log re-renders on every streamed token, and only the last message
// changes. Upserts keep unchanged messages and results by reference, so a
// reference check per message and per tool call is enough.
function sameResults(m: Message, a: Props['results'], b: Props['results']): boolean {
  for (const p of m.parts) if (p.type === 'tool_call' && a.get(p.data.id) !== b.get(p.data.id)) return false;
  return true;
}

export class MessageView extends Component<Props> {
  shouldComponentUpdate(next: Props) {
    return next.m !== this.props.m || !sameResults(next.m, this.props.results, next.results);
  }
  render() {
    return <MessageViewBody m={this.props.m} results={this.props.results} />;
  }
}
```

and its `image_url` case:

```tsx
          case 'image_url':
            return isLocalUrl(p.data.url)
              ? <img key={i} class="msg__image" src={p.data.url} alt="Attached image" />
              : <a key={i} href={p.data.url} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Image: {p.data.url}</a>;
```

with `export const isLocalUrl = (u: string) => u.startsWith('data:') || u.startsWith('blob:');`
in `Markdown.tsx`, whose hook gains:

```ts
  // img-src only allows data: and blob:, so a remote image would render as
  // a broken box; a link keeps the reference usable.
  if (node.tagName === 'IMG') {
    const src = node.getAttribute('src') ?? '';
    if (!isLocalUrl(src)) {
      const a = node.ownerDocument.createElement('a');
      a.setAttribute('href', src);
      a.setAttribute('target', '_blank');
      a.setAttribute('rel', 'noopener noreferrer');
      a.textContent = `Image: ${node.getAttribute('alt') || src}`;
      node.replaceWith(a);
    }
  }
```

`Transcript.tsx` takes the body of today's `TransmissionLog`:

```tsx
import type { ComponentChildren } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import type { Message } from '../../api/types';
import { toolResults } from '../../state/reducer';
import { MessageView } from './MessageView';

const WINDOW = 200;
const PIN_SLACK = 80;

export function Transcript({ messages, resetKey, label, empty }: {
  messages: Message[]; resetKey: string; label: string; empty?: ComponentChildren;
}) {
  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(WINDOW);
  const scroller = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const pinned = useRef(true);
  const lastUser = useRef<string | undefined>(undefined);
  const results = useMemo(() => toolResults(messages), [messages]);
  const visible = useMemo(() => messages.filter((m) => m.role !== 'tool'), [messages]);
  const shown = visible.slice(-limit);

  useEffect(() => {
    setLimit(WINDOW);
    pinned.current = true;
    lastUser.current = undefined;
  }, [resetKey]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Sending is a request to see the answer, so a new user message pins
    // the view again even when the user had scrolled up.
    const last = visible.at(-1);
    if (last?.role === 'user' && last.id !== lastUser.current) {
      lastUser.current = last.id;
      pinned.current = true;
    }
    const el = scroller.current;
    if (el && pinned.current) el.scrollTop = el.scrollHeight;
  }, [messages]);

  const onScroll = () => {
    const el = scroller.current;
    if (el) pinned.current = el.scrollHeight - el.scrollTop - el.clientHeight < PIN_SLACK;
  };

  return (
    <div class="log" ref={scroller} onScroll={onScroll} role="log" aria-live="off" aria-label={label}>
      {visible.length > shown.length && (
        <button type="button" class="link-button log__earlier" onClick={() => setLimit((l) => l + WINDOW)}>
          Show {Math.min(WINDOW, visible.length - shown.length)} earlier messages
        </button>
      )}
      {shown.length === 0 && empty}
      {shown.map((m) => <MessageView key={m.id} m={m} results={results} />)}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`TransmissionLog.tsx` becomes:

```tsx
export function TransmissionLog() {
  const { messages, sessionId, conn } = state.value;
  return (
    <Transcript
      messages={messages}
      resetKey={sessionId}
      label="Transmissions"
      empty={conn !== 'booting' && (
        <div class="log__empty">
          <p class="hud-label">No transmissions yet</p>
          <p>Type a task in the deck below or in the terminal. Replies, tool calls and the agent's thought layer stream in here.</p>
        </div>
      )}
    />
  );
}
```

`toolResults` replaces a result map entry only when a tool message changes,
but it rebuilds the `Map` itself; `sameResults` compares entries, so that is
enough. Existing `TransmissionLog.test.tsx` tests keep passing unchanged;
fix only selector drift (the log now carries `aria-label="Transmissions"`).

as the nodes") and the 2D fallback of spec 3.4. Deferred G1-1 and G2-10.
The list is a `listbox`: one tab stop, arrows move, Enter or Space selects,
and selection is the inspected agent.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { ORBIT, orbit2D, orbitAngle, orbitPoint } from './orbit';

describe('orbit', () => {
  it('spaces nodes evenly and drifts with time', () => {
    expect(orbitAngle(1, 4, 0, ORBIT.speed) - orbitAngle(0, 4, 0, ORBIT.speed)).toBeCloseTo(Math.PI / 2);
    expect(orbitAngle(0, 4, 10, ORBIT.speed)).toBeCloseTo(orbitAngle(0, 4, 0, ORBIT.speed) + 10 * ORBIT.speed);

      const p = orbitPoint(a);
      expect(Math.hypot(p.x, p.y - ORBIT.height, p.z)).toBeCloseTo(ORBIT.radius);
      expect(Math.abs(p.y - ORBIT.height)).toBeLessThanOrEqual(ORBIT.radius * Math.sin(ORBIT.tilt) + 1e-9);

});
```

```tsx
    await act(() => { key(options()[1], 'End'); });

    await act(() => { render(<AgentList />, root); });

    expect(options().find((o) => o.tabIndex === 0)?.textContent).toContain('alice');
  });

  it('says the status in text', async () => {
    await act(() => { render(<AgentList />, root); });
    expect(options()[1].textContent).toContain('Running');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `orbit.ts`**

```ts
// The agent orbit, shared by the three.js nodes and the 2D fallback. No
// three import, so the eager 2D view can use it.
export const ORBIT = { radius: 2.7, tilt: 0.32, height: 1.6, speed: 0.08 };

export function orbitAngle(index: number, count: number, elapsed: number, speed: number): number {
  return -Math.PI / 2 + (index * 2 * Math.PI) / Math.max(1, count) + elapsed * speed;
}

// orbitPoint puts an angle on a ring tilted toward the camera around the
// core, so nodes in front sit lower than nodes behind.
export function orbitPoint(angle: number): { x: number; y: number; z: number } {
  const x = Math.cos(angle) * ORBIT.radius;
  const flat = Math.sin(angle) * ORBIT.radius;
  return { x, y: ORBIT.height - flat * Math.sin(ORBIT.tilt), z: flat * Math.cos(ORBIT.tilt) };
}

// orbit2D places a node on the outer ring of the 2D constellation
// (ellipse rx 150, ry 34 at cy 70 in the SVG viewBox).
export function orbit2D(index: number, count: number): { x: number; y: number } {
  const a = orbitAngle(index, count, 0, 0) + Math.PI;
  return { x: 150 * Math.cos(a), y: 70 + 34 * Math.sin(a) };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `AgentList.tsx`**

```tsx
import { useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { agentsOf, statusLabel, type Agent } from '../../state/agents';
import { needsAttention } from '../../state/reducer';
import { actions, state } from '../../state/store';

const GLYPH: Record<Agent['kind'], string> = { main: '⬡', teammate: '⬢', subagent: '◈' };

function label(a: Agent, attention: boolean): string {
  if (a.kind === 'main') return attention ? 'Waiting for you' : a.status === 'running' ? 'Working' : 'Idle';
  return statusLabel(a.status);
}

export function AgentList() {
  const s = state.value;
  const agents = agentsOf(s);
  const attention = needsAttention(s);
  const selected = s.inspected?.sessionId ?? null;
  // The tab stop follows an agent, not an index, so it stays on the same
  // agent when others join or leave the roster.
  const [activeId, setActiveId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const items = useRef(new Map<string, HTMLLIElement>());
  const found = agents.findIndex((a) => a.sessionId === (activeId ?? selected));
  const index = found < 0 ? 0 : found;

  const move = (to: number) => {
    const next = agents[(to + agents.length) % agents.length];
    setActiveId(next.sessionId);
    items.current.get(next.sessionId)?.focus();
  };
  const choose = (a: Agent) => {
    setActiveId(a.sessionId);
    void actions.inspect(a.sessionId || null);
  };
  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    switch (e.key) {
      case 'ArrowDown':
      case 'ArrowRight':
        e.preventDefault();
        move(index + 1);
        break;
      case 'ArrowUp':
      case 'ArrowLeft':
        e.preventDefault();
        move(index - 1);
        break;
      case 'Home':
        e.preventDefault();
        move(0);
        break;
      case 'End':
        e.preventDefault();
        move(agents.length - 1);
        break;
      case 'Enter':
      case ' ':
        e.preventDefault();
        choose(agents[index]);
        break;
    }
  };

  return (
    <ul class="agent-list" role="listbox" aria-label="Agents" onKeyDown={onKeyDown}>
      {agents.map((a, i) => (
        <li
          key={a.sessionId || 'main'}
          id={`agent-${a.sessionId || 'main'}`}
          ref={(el) => { if (el) items.current.set(a.sessionId, el); else items.current.delete(a.sessionId); }}
          role="option"
          tabIndex={i === index ? 0 : -1}
          aria-selected={a.sessionId === selected}
          data-kind={a.kind}
          data-status={a.kind === 'main' && attention ? 'attention' : a.status}
          onClick={() => choose(a)}
        >
          <span class="agent-list__glyph" aria-hidden="true">{GLYPH[a.kind]}</span>
          <span class="agent-list__name">{a.name}</span>
          <span class="hud-label agent-list__status">{label(a, attention)}</span>
        </li>
      ))}
    </ul>
  );
}
```

`ConstellationSvg.tsx` gains, after the core glow:

```tsx
      {agents.map((a, i) => {
        const p = orbit2D(i, agents.length);
        return (
          <g key={a.id} class="cs-node" data-agent-node={a.id} data-kind={a.kind} data-status={a.status}
            data-selected={a.id === selected} transform={`translate(${p.x} ${p.y})`} onClick={() => onSelect(a.id)}>
            <line class="cs-filament" x1={-p.x} y1={-20 - p.y} x2="0" y2="0" />
            <polygon class="cs-node__hex" points="0,-11 9.5,-5.5 9.5,5.5 0,11 -9.5,5.5 -9.5,-5.5" />
            {a.status === 'failed' && <text class="cs-node__icon" y="4">!</text>}
          </g>
        );
      })}
```

The `transform` here is an SVG presentation attribute, not a `style`
attribute, so the CSP check stays at 0. The `<svg>` drops `role="img"` and
`aria-label` for `aria-hidden="true"`: the listbox is its DOM equivalent.

`agents.css` (new; add `'agents.css'` to `EXPECTED_SHEETS` and import it in
`main.tsx` after `stage.css`) moves the `.agent-list` rules out of
`stage.css` and adds:

```css
.agent-list li { cursor: pointer; }
.agent-list li[aria-selected='true'] { border-color: var(--cyan); box-shadow: var(--glow-cyan); }
.agent-list li[data-kind='subagent'] .agent-list__glyph { color: var(--violet); }
.agent-list li[data-kind='teammate'] .agent-list__glyph { color: var(--cyan); }
.agent-list li[data-status='inactive'], .agent-list li[data-status='done'] { opacity: 0.7; }
.agent-list li[data-status='failed'] { border-color: var(--magenta); }
.agent-list__status { margin-inline-start: auto; }
.cs-node { cursor: pointer; pointer-events: auto; }
.cs-node__hex { fill: var(--void-deep); stroke: var(--cyan); stroke-width: 1.5; }
.cs-node[data-kind='subagent'] .cs-node__hex { stroke: var(--violet); }
.cs-node[data-status='inactive'] .cs-node__hex, .cs-node[data-status='done'] .cs-node__hex { opacity: 0.4; }
.cs-node[data-status='failed'] .cs-node__hex { stroke: var(--magenta); }
.cs-node[data-selected='true'] .cs-node__hex { stroke-width: 3; }
.cs-node__icon { fill: var(--magenta); font: 700 10px var(--font-hud); text-anchor: middle; }
.cs-filament { stroke: var(--cyan); stroke-opacity: 0.35; }
.cs-node[data-kind='subagent'] .cs-filament { stroke: var(--violet); }
```

### Task 14: Scene agent nodes, filaments and targeted bursts

Spec 3.4 "Agent nodes" and 3.10 ("In-scene agent labels re-render only when
their content changes"). Nodes and filaments are three.js; the clickable
labels are DOM tags positioned through the CSSOM from projected node
positions, so a pointer click and a Playwright click hit a real element, and
moving tags never re-render Preact.

**Files:**
- Create: `web/src/scene/agents.ts`, `web/src/scene/agents.test.ts`
- Modify: `web/src/scene/bursts.ts` (`spawnAt`), `web/src/scene/bursts.test.ts`
- Modify: `web/src/scene/mount.ts` (`SceneInput.agents`, `selected`, `onAgentScreens`, `burst(c, target?)`), `web/src/scene/mount.test.ts`
- Modify: `web/src/scene/input.ts`
- Modify: `web/src/state/layout.ts` (`AgentScreen`, `publishAgentScreens`, `subscribeAgentScreens`, `currentAgentScreens`)
- Create: `web/src/ui/stage/AgentTags.tsx`, `web/src/ui/stage/AgentTags.test.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/ui/stage/SceneLayer.tsx`, `SceneLayer.test.tsx`, `Stage.tsx`
- Modify: `web/src/styles/agents.css`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ORBIT`, `orbitAngle`, `orbitPoint` (Task 13); `deriveAgents`, `statusLabel` (Task 10); `State.activity` (Task 10).
- Produces:
  - `scene/agents.ts`: `interface SceneAgent { id: string; kind: 'teammate' | 'subagent'; status: AgentStatus }`, `createAgentNodes(colors: SceneColors, core: THREE.Vector3): AgentNodes` with `group`, `sync(agents, selected)`, `update(dt, elapsed, motion): boolean`, `position(id, out: THREE.Vector3): boolean`, `pulse(id, color)`, `flash(id, color)`, `ids()`, `dispose()`
  - `Bursts.spawnAt(c: ToolCategory, at: THREE.Vector3): void`
  - `SceneInput` gains `agents: SceneAgent[]` and `selected: string | null`; `SceneHandle.burst(c: ToolCategory, target?: string)`; mount hooks gain `onAgentScreens?(screens: AgentScreen[]): void`
  - `state/layout.ts`: `interface AgentScreen { id: string; x: number; y: number; visible: boolean }` (viewport CSS pixels), `publishAgentScreens(s)`, `subscribeAgentScreens(fn): () => void`, `currentAgentScreens(): AgentScreen[]`
  - `AgentTags({ agents, selected })`: `aria-hidden` hologram tags, each `[data-agent-node=<sessionId>]` with `data-visible`

`scene/agents.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { createAgentNodes, type SceneAgent } from './agents';
import { readColors } from './colors';
import { ORBIT } from './orbit';

const CORE = new THREE.Vector3(0, ORBIT.height, 0);
const teammate: SceneAgent = { id: 'team-m-1', kind: 'teammate', status: 'running' };
const subagent: SceneAgent = { id: 'sub-1', kind: 'subagent', status: 'running' };

function nodes() {
  const colors = readColors();
  return { colors, n: createAgentNodes(colors, CORE) };
}

describe('agent nodes', () => {
  it('builds one prism and one filament per agent, colored by kind', () => {
    const { colors, n } = nodes();
    n.sync([teammate, subagent], null);
    expect(n.ids().sort()).toEqual(['sub-1', 'team-m-1']);
    const bodies = n.group.children.filter((c) => c.name === 'agent-node') as THREE.Mesh[];
    expect(bodies).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(n.group.children.filter((c) => c.name === 'agent-filament')).toHaveLength(2);
    const color = (id: string) => ((bodies.find((b) => b.userData.id === id)!.material) as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial).color;
    expect(color('team-m-1').getHex()).toBe(colors.cyan.getHex());
    expect(color('sub-1').getHex()).toBe(colors.violet.getHex());
  });

  it('dims an inactive node and shows only the wireframe while provisioning', () => {
    const { n } = nodes();
    n.sync([{ ...teammate, status: 'inactive' }, { ...subagent, status: 'provisioning' }], null);
    n.update(0.016, 0, 'full');
    const body = (id: string) => n.group.children.find((c) => c.name === 'agent-node' && c.userData.id === id) as THREE.Mesh;
    expect((body('team-m-1').material as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial).opacity).toBeLessThan(0.5);
    expect(body('sub-1').visible).toBe(false);
    expect(n.group.children.find((c) => c.name === 'agent-wire' && c.userData.id === 'sub-1')!.visible).toBe(true);
  });

  it('turns a failed node magenta', () => {
    const { colors, n } = nodes();
    n.sync([{ ...teammate, status: 'failed' }], null);
    n.update(0.016, 0, 'full');
    const body = n.group.children.find((c) => c.name === 'agent-node') as THREE.Mesh;
    expect((body.material as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial).color.getHex()).toBe(colors.magenta.getHex());
  });

  it('collapses a node that leaves the roster into the core, then removes it', () => {
    const { n } = nodes();
    n.sync([teammate], null);
    n.update(0.016, 0, 'full');
    n.sync([], null);
    expect(n.update(0.3, 0.3, 'full')).toBe(true);
    const p = new THREE.Vector3();
    expect(n.position('team-m-1', p)).toBe(false);
    for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) n.update(0.1, 1 + i * 0.1, 'full');
    expect(n.ids()).toEqual([]);
    expect(n.group.children.filter((c) => c.name === 'agent-node')).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  it('holds still with motion off and drifts in full motion', () => {
    const { n } = nodes();
    n.sync([teammate], null);
    const a = new THREE.Vector3();
    const b = new THREE.Vector3();
    n.update(0.016, 0, 'off');
    n.position('team-m-1', a);
    n.update(0.016, 30, 'off');
    n.position('team-m-1', b);
    expect(b.distanceTo(a)).toBe(0);
    n.update(0.016, 30, 'full');
    n.position('team-m-1', b);
    expect(b.distanceTo(a)).toBeGreaterThan(0.1);
  });

  it('reports a pulse as animation until it lands', () => {
    const { colors, n } = nodes();
    n.sync([teammate], null);
    n.update(0.016, 0, 'off');
    n.pulse('team-m-1', colors.teal);
    expect(n.update(0.1, 0.1, 'off')).toBe(true);
    for (let i = 0; i < 20; i++) n.update(0.1, 0.2 + i * 0.1, 'off');
    expect(n.update(0.1, 3, 'off')).toBe(false);
  });

  it('enlarges the selected node', () => {
    const { n } = nodes();
    n.sync([teammate, subagent], 'sub-1');
    n.update(0.016, 0, 'off');
    const scale = (id: string) => n.group.children.find((c) => c.name === 'agent-node' && c.userData.id === id)!.scale.x;
    expect(scale('sub-1')).toBeGreaterThan(scale('team-m-1'));
  });

  it('disposes its geometries and materials and empties the group', () => {
    const { n } = nodes();
    n.sync([teammate, subagent], null);
    const disposed: string[] = [];
    n.group.traverse((o) => {
      const m = o as THREE.Mesh;
      if (m.geometry) m.geometry.addEventListener('dispose', () => disposed.push('g'));
      if (m.material) (m.material as THREE.Material).addEventListener('dispose', () => disposed.push('m'));
    });
    n.dispose();
    expect(disposed.filter((d) => d === 'm').length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(6);
    expect(disposed).toContain('g');
    expect(n.group.children).toHaveLength(0);
  });
});
```

```ts
  it('syncs the nodes only when the roster or the selection changes', () => {
    const scene = start({ motion: 'off' });
    const agents = parts.agents.at(-1)!;
    agents.sync.mockClear();
    scene.update(input({ motion: 'off', agents: [{ id: 'a1', kind: 'teammate', status: 'running' }] }));

    scene.update(input({ motion: 'off', agents: [{ id: 'a1', kind: 'teammate', status: 'running' }] }));

    scene.update(input({ motion: 'off', agents: [{ id: 'a1', kind: 'teammate', status: 'running' }], selected: 'a1' }));
    expect(agents.sync).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it('publishes projected node positions after a render, and again only when they move', () => {
    const screens = vi.fn();
    const scene = start({ motion: 'off' }, { onAgentScreens: screens });
    scene.update(input({ motion: 'off', agents: [{ id: 'a1', kind: 'teammate', status: 'running' }] }));
    advance(1);
    expect(screens).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(screens.mock.calls[0][0]).toEqual([expect.objectContaining({ id: 'a1', visible: true })]);
    scene.requestFrame();
    advance(1);
    expect(screens).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it('bursts toward a node in full motion and flashes it in reduced motion', () => {
    const full = start({ motion: 'full' });
    full.burst('write', 'a1');
    expect(parts.bursts.at(-1)!.spawnAt).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(parts.agents.at(-1)!.pulse).toHaveBeenCalledWith('a1', expect.anything());
    full.dispose();
    const reduced = start({ motion: 'reduced' });
    reduced.burst('write', 'a1');
    expect(parts.agents.at(-1)!.flash).toHaveBeenCalledWith('a1', expect.anything());
  });
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `scene/agents.ts`**

```ts
import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { AgentStatus } from '../state/agents';
import type { Motion } from '../state/motion';
import type { SceneColors } from './colors';
import { motionScale } from './motionScale';
import { ORBIT, orbitAngle, orbitPoint } from './orbit';

export interface SceneAgent {
  id: string;
  kind: 'teammate' | 'subagent';
  status: AgentStatus;
}

export interface AgentNodes {
  group: THREE.Group;
  sync(agents: SceneAgent[], selected: string | null): void;
  update(dt: number, elapsed: number, motion: Motion): boolean;
  position(id: string, out: THREE.Vector3): boolean;
  pulse(id: string, color: THREE.Color): void;
  flash(id: string, color: THREE.Color): void;
  ids(): string[];
  dispose(): void;
}

const RADIUS = 0.2;
const HEIGHT = 0.3;
const SPIN = 0.6;
const COLLAPSE_S = 0.6;
const PULSE_S = 0.7;
const FLASH_S = 0.5;
const FLICKER_S = 0.09;
const SELECTED_SCALE = 1.3;
const DIM = 0.35;

interface Node {
  agent: SceneAgent;
  index: number;
  body: THREE.Mesh;
  material: THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
  wire: THREE.LineSegments;
  wireMaterial: THREE.LineBasicMaterial;
  filament: THREE.Line;
  filamentMaterial: THREE.LineBasicMaterial;
  spark: THREE.Mesh;
  sparkMaterial: THREE.MeshBasicMaterial;
  pulseAge: number;
  flashAge: number;
  flashColor: THREE.Color;
  // Seconds into the collapse once the node left the roster or finished;
  // negative while it is on the orbit.
  collapse: number;
  selected: boolean;
  // listed is false once the roster no longer carries the agent; such a
  // node is removed when its collapse ends.
  listed: boolean;
}

export function createAgentNodes(colors: SceneColors, core: THREE.Vector3): AgentNodes {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  group.name = 'agents';
  const prism = new THREE.CylinderGeometry(RADIUS, RADIUS, HEIGHT, 6);
  const edges = new THREE.EdgesGeometry(prism);
  const sparkGeometry = new THREE.SphereGeometry(0.05, 8, 8);
  const nodes = new Map<string, Node>();
  let count = 0;

  const kindColor = (a: SceneAgent) => (a.kind === 'teammate' ? colors.cyan : colors.violet);

  const build = (agent: SceneAgent): Node => {
    const material = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: kindColor(agent).clone(), transparent: true, opacity: 1, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false });
    const body = new THREE.Mesh(prism, material);
    body.name = 'agent-node';
    body.userData.id = agent.id;
    const wireMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: kindColor(agent).clone(), transparent: true, opacity: 0.9 });
    const wire = new THREE.LineSegments(edges, wireMaterial);
    wire.name = 'agent-wire';
    wire.userData.id = agent.id;
    const filamentGeometry = new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([core.clone(), core.clone()]);
    const filamentMaterial = new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: kindColor(agent).clone(), transparent: true, opacity: 0.35, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false });
    const filament = new THREE.Line(filamentGeometry, filamentMaterial);
    filament.name = 'agent-filament';
    filament.userData.id = agent.id;
    const sparkMaterial = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ color: colors.cyan.clone(), transparent: true, opacity: 0, blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending, depthWrite: false });
    const spark = new THREE.Mesh(sparkGeometry, sparkMaterial);
    spark.name = 'agent-spark';
    spark.visible = false;
    group.add(body, wire, filament, spark);
    return {
      agent, index: 0, body, material, wire, wireMaterial, filament, filamentMaterial, spark, sparkMaterial,
      pulseAge: Infinity, flashAge: Infinity, flashColor: new THREE.Color(), collapse: -1, selected: false, listed: true,
    };
  };

  const remove = (id: string, n: Node) => {
    group.remove(n.body, n.wire, n.filament, n.spark);
    n.material.dispose();
    n.wireMaterial.dispose();
    n.filamentMaterial.dispose();
    n.filament.geometry.dispose();
    n.sparkMaterial.dispose();
    nodes.delete(id);
  };

  const sync = (agents: SceneAgent[], selected: string | null) => {
    const present = new Set(agents.map((a) => a.id));
    agents.forEach((agent, index) => {
      const n = nodes.get(agent.id) ?? build(agent);
      nodes.set(agent.id, n);
      n.agent = agent;
      n.index = index;
      n.listed = true;
      n.selected = agent.id === selected;
      if (agent.status === 'done' && n.collapse < 0) n.collapse = 0;
      if (agent.status !== 'done') n.collapse = -1;
    });
    for (const [id, n] of nodes) {
      if (present.has(id)) continue;
      n.listed = false;
      if (n.collapse < 0) n.collapse = 0;
    }
    count = agents.length;
  };

  const tmp = new THREE.Vector3();

  const update = (dt: number, elapsed: number, motion: Motion): boolean => {
    let animating = false;
    const drift = elapsed * motionScale(motion);
    for (const [id, n] of [...nodes]) {
      const { agent } = n;
      const p = orbitPoint(orbitAngle(n.index, Math.max(1, count), drift, ORBIT.speed));
      tmp.set(p.x, p.y, p.z);
      let scale = n.selected ? SELECTED_SCALE : 1;
      if (n.collapse >= 0) {
        n.collapse += dt;
        const t = Math.min(1, n.collapse / COLLAPSE_S);
        tmp.lerp(core, t);
        scale *= 1 - t;
        if (t >= 1) {
          // A finished agent still on the roster stays collapsed; one that
          // left it is gone.
          if (!n.listed) {
            remove(id, n);
            continue;
          }
        } else {
          animating = true;
        }
      }
      n.body.position.copy(tmp);
      n.wire.position.copy(tmp);
      n.body.scale.setScalar(scale);
      n.wire.scale.setScalar(scale);
      if (agent.status === 'running' && motion !== 'off') {
        n.body.rotation.y += dt * SPIN * motionScale(motion);
        n.wire.rotation.y = n.body.rotation.y;
      }
      const failed = agent.status === 'failed';
      const base = failed ? colors.magenta : kindColor(agent);
      n.material.color.copy(base);
      n.wireMaterial.color.copy(base);
      if (n.flashAge < FLASH_S) {
        n.flashAge += dt;
        n.material.color.lerp(n.flashColor, 1 - Math.min(1, n.flashAge / FLASH_S));
        animating = true;
      }
      const collapsed = n.collapse >= COLLAPSE_S;
      n.body.visible = !collapsed && agent.status !== 'provisioning';
      n.wire.visible = !collapsed && (agent.status === 'provisioning' || n.selected);
      n.material.opacity = agent.status === 'inactive' || agent.status === 'done' ? DIM : 1;
      if (agent.status === 'provisioning') {
        n.wireMaterial.opacity = 0.5 + 0.4 * Math.sin(elapsed * 4);
        if (motion !== 'off') animating = true;
      }
      if (failed && motion === 'full') {
        n.body.visible = n.body.visible && Math.floor(elapsed / FLICKER_S) % 7 !== 0;
        animating = true;
      }
      const line = n.filament.geometry.getAttribute('position') as THREE.BufferAttribute;
      line.setXYZ(1, tmp.x, tmp.y, tmp.z);
      line.needsUpdate = true;
      n.filament.visible = !collapsed;
      if (n.pulseAge < PULSE_S) {
        n.pulseAge += dt;
        const t = Math.min(1, n.pulseAge / PULSE_S);
        n.spark.visible = t < 1;
        n.spark.position.lerpVectors(core, tmp, t);
        n.sparkMaterial.opacity = 1 - t;
        n.filamentMaterial.opacity = 0.35 + 0.5 * (1 - t);
        animating = animating || t < 1;
      } else {
        n.spark.visible = false;
        n.filamentMaterial.opacity = 0.35;
      }
    }
    return animating;
  };

  const position = (id: string, out: THREE.Vector3): boolean => {
    const n = nodes.get(id);
    if (!n || n.collapse >= 0) return false;
    out.copy(n.body.position);
    return true;
  };

  const pulse = (id: string, color: THREE.Color) => {
    const n = nodes.get(id);
    if (!n) return;
    n.pulseAge = 0;
    n.sparkMaterial.color.copy(color);
  };

  const flash = (id: string, color: THREE.Color) => {
    const n = nodes.get(id);
    if (!n) return;
    n.flashAge = 0;
    n.flashColor.copy(color);
  };

  const dispose = () => {
    for (const [id, n] of [...nodes]) remove(id, n);
    prism.dispose();
    edges.dispose();
    sparkGeometry.dispose();
    group.clear();
  };

  return { group, sync, update, position, pulse, flash, ids: () => [...nodes.keys()], dispose };
}
```

`bursts.ts`: `spawn` resets `ring.mesh.position.set(0, 0, 0)`; add

```ts
  // spawnAt rings a node instead of the core. The pool lives in the core's
  // frame, so the node position is made relative to it.
  const spawnAt = (c: ToolCategory, at: THREE.Vector3) => {
    spawn(c);
    const ring = rings.reduce((a, b) => (b.born > a.born ? b : a));
    ring.mesh.position.copy(at).sub(group.position);
  };
```

and return it from `createBursts` (`Bursts` interface gains `spawnAt`).

- [ ] **Step 4: Mount, input, layout**

`state/layout.ts`:

```ts
// Agent node positions in viewport pixels, written by the scene after a
// render. Tags apply them through the CSSOM, so a moving node never
// re-renders the page.
export interface AgentScreen { id: string; x: number; y: number; visible: boolean }

let lastScreens: AgentScreen[] = [];
let screenSink: ((s: AgentScreen[]) => void) | null = null;

export function publishAgentScreens(screens: AgentScreen[]): void {
  lastScreens = screens;
  screenSink?.(screens);
}

export function subscribeAgentScreens(fn: (s: AgentScreen[]) => void): () => void {
  screenSink = fn;
  fn(lastScreens);
  return () => {
    if (screenSink === fn) screenSink = null;
  };
}

export function currentAgentScreens(): AgentScreen[] {
  return lastScreens;
}
```

`scene/input.ts` adds to `sameSceneInput`:

```ts

    a.agents.length === b.agents.length &&
    a.agents.every((x, i) => x.id === b.agents[i].id && x.kind === b.agents[i].kind && x.status === b.agents[i].status) &&
```

`mount.ts`:
- `SceneInput` gains `agents: SceneAgent[]; selected: string | null;`
  (type import from `./agents`).
- The hooks type gains `onAgentScreens?(screens: AgentScreen[]): void`
  (type import from `../state/layout`).
- After the core: `const agents = createAgentNodes(colors, core.emissive.getWorldPosition(new THREE.Vector3())); scene.add(agents.group); agents.sync(input.agents, input.selected);`
- In `frame`, after `burstsAlive = bursts.update(dt);`:
  `const nodesAnimating = agents.update(dt, elapsed, motion);` and add
  `nodesAnimating ||` to `eventActive`.
- After the render call: `publishScreens();` with

```ts
  let lastScreens = '';
  const projected = new THREE.Vector3();
  // publishScreens projects every node after a render and reports only a
  // change of at least a pixel, so idle frames cost the page nothing.
  const publishScreens = () => {
    if (!hooks.onAgentScreens) return;
    const width = canvas.clientWidth;
    const height = canvas.clientHeight;
    const screens: AgentScreen[] = [];
    for (const id of agents.ids()) {
      if (!agents.position(id, projected)) continue;
      projected.project(camera);
      screens.push({
        id,
        x: Math.round(((projected.x + 1) / 2) * width),
        y: Math.round(((1 - projected.y) / 2) * height),
        visible: projected.z < 1,
      });
    }
    const key = JSON.stringify(screens);
    if (key === lastScreens) return;
    lastScreens = key;
    hooks.onAgentScreens(screens);
  };
```

- In `update`: `if (next.selected !== input.selected || !sameAgentList(next.agents, input.agents)) agents.sync(next.agents, next.selected);`
  (`sameAgentList` is the agent half of `sameSceneInput`; export it from
  `input.ts` and use it in both places.)
- `burst` becomes:

```ts
  const target = new THREE.Vector3();
  const burst = (c: ToolCategory, agentId?: string) => {
    const color = categoryColor(colors, c);
    if (agentId && agents.position(agentId, target)) {
      if (input.motion === 'full') {
        bursts.spawnAt(c, target);
        agents.pulse(agentId, color);
      } else if (input.motion === 'reduced') {
        agents.flash(agentId, color);
      }
    } else if (input.motion === 'full') {
      bursts.spawn(c);
    } else if (input.motion === 'reduced') {
      core.flash(color);
    }
    kick();
  };
```

- `dispose` calls `agents.dispose()` next to `bursts.dispose()`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Tags and the scene layer**

`AgentTags.tsx`:

```tsx
import { useLayoutEffect, useRef } from 'preact/hooks';
import { statusLabel, type Agent } from '../../state/agents';
import { currentAgentScreens, subscribeAgentScreens, type AgentScreen } from '../../state/layout';
import { actions } from '../../state/store';

// The tags are the pointer targets of the 3D nodes. They are hidden from
// assistive technology: the Agents listbox is the accessible path.
export function AgentTags({ agents, selected }: { agents: Agent[]; selected: string | null }) {
  const root = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const apply = (screens: AgentScreen[]) => {
    const el = root.current;
    if (!el) return;
    for (const s of screens) {
      const tag = el.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-agent-node="${CSS.escape(s.id)}"]`);
      if (!tag) continue;
      tag.style.transform = `translate(${s.x}px, ${s.y}px) translate(-50%, -140%)`;
      tag.dataset.visible = String(s.visible);
    }
  };
  useLayoutEffect(() => subscribeAgentScreens(apply), []);
  const ids = agents.map((a) => a.sessionId).join('|');
  // A tag that just mounted has no position yet; give it the last one.
  useLayoutEffect(() => apply(currentAgentScreens()), [ids]);
  return (
    <div class="agent-tags" ref={root} aria-hidden="true">
      {agents.map((a) => (
        <div
          key={a.sessionId}
          class="agent-tag holo"
          data-agent-node={a.sessionId}
          data-kind={a.kind}
          data-status={a.status}
          data-selected={a.sessionId === selected}
          data-visible="false"
          onClick={() => void actions.inspect(a.sessionId)}
        >
          <span class="agent-tag__name">{a.name}</span>
          <span class="agent-tag__status">{statusLabel(a.status)}</span>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';

const agent: Agent = { sessionId: 'sub-1', parentSessionId: 's1', name: 'Map the parser', kind: 'subagent', status: 'running', model: '', modelProvider: '', startedAt: 0, finishedAt: 0 };

describe('AgentTags', () => {

    const tag = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-agent-node="sub-1"]')!;

    expect(tag.style.transform).toContain('translate(300px, 200px)');

    await act(() => { render(<AgentTags agents={[agent]} selected={null} />, root); });

```

(The `getAttribute('style')` line documents that CSSOM writes show up as a
style attribute at runtime, which the CSP allows; only markup attributes
are blocked. The dist `grep -c 'style='` check covers markup.)

`SceneLayer.tsx`:
- `input` gains `agents: deriveAgents(s.sessionId, s.team, s.runs).map((a) => ({ id: a.sessionId, kind: a.kind as SceneAgent['kind'], status: a.status }))`
  and `selected: s.inspected?.sessionId ?? null`.
- `mountScene` hooks gain `onAgentScreens: publishAgentScreens`.
- A ref `lastCalls` holds the call id per session. The mount `then` seeds it
  from `state.peek().activity`; a new effect on `[s.activity]` bursts:

```ts
  useEffect(() => {
    const handle = scene.current;
    const seen = lastCalls.current;
    lastCalls.current = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(s.activity).map(([sid, c]) => [sid, c.callId]));
    if (!handle) return;
    for (const [sid, call] of Object.entries(s.activity)) {
      if (seen[sid] !== call.callId) handle.burst(toolCategory(call.tool), sid);
    }
  }, [s.activity]);
```

```tsx
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render } from 'preact';
import { act } from 'preact/test-utils';
import fixtures from '../../state/__fixtures__/events.json';

const fx = fixtures as unknown as Record<string, Frame>;

document.body.append(root);

function open(sid: string, messages: Message[] = []) {
  const s = inspect(reduce(initialState(), fx.snapshot), sid);
  state.value = { ...s, inspected: { ...s.inspected!, messages, loading: false } };
}

beforeEach(() => {

  vi.useRealTimers();
  vi.restoreAllMocks();

  it('is hidden without a selection', async () => {
    state.value = reduce(initialState(), fx.snapshot);
    await act(() => { render(<Inspector />, root); });
    expect(region().hidden).toBe(true);
  });

  it('shows what a subagent is doing and cancels it', async () => {
    open('sub-1', [child]);
    const cancel = vi.spyOn(actions, 'cancelAgent').mockResolvedValue(null);
    await act(() => { render(<Inspector />, root); });
    expect(region().hidden).toBe(false);
    expect(root.querySelector('#inspector-title')!.textContent).toBe('Map the parser');
    expect(region().textContent).toContain('Subagent');
    expect(region().textContent).toContain('Running');
    expect(region().querySelector('[data-fact="tool"]')!.textContent).toContain('grep');
    expect(region().querySelector('[data-fact="tool"]')!.textContent).toContain('42s');
    expect(region().querySelector('[role="log"]')!.textContent).toContain('Scanning the tokenizer.');
    await act(() => { root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button[data-action="cancel"]')!.click(); });
    expect(cancel).toHaveBeenCalledWith('sub-1');
  });

  it('shows a teammate task and model, interrupts it and sends a message', async () => {
    open('team-m-1');
    const interrupt = vi.spyOn(actions, 'interruptTeammate').mockResolvedValue(null);
    const send = vi.spyOn(actions, 'messageTeammate').mockResolvedValue(null);
    await act(() => { render(<Inspector />, root); });
    expect(region().querySelector('[data-fact="task"]')!.textContent).toContain('Port the parser');
    expect(region().querySelector('[data-fact="model"]')!.textContent).toContain('claude');
    await act(async () => { root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button[data-action="interrupt"]')!.click(); });
    expect(interrupt).toHaveBeenCalledWith('alice');
    const box = root.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>('textarea')!;
    await act(() => {
      box.value = 'rebase first';
      box.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
    });
    await act(async () => { root.querySelector<HTMLFormElement>('form')!.dispatchEvent(new Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true })); });
    expect(send).toHaveBeenCalledWith('alice', 'rebase first');
    expect(box.value).toBe('');
    expect(region().querySelector('[role="status"]')!.textContent).toContain('Sent to alice');
  });

  it('shows a refused action inline', async () => {
    open('team-m-1');
    vi.spyOn(actions, 'interruptTeammate').mockResolvedValue('Agent Teams is not enabled');
    await act(() => { render(<Inspector />, root); });
    await act(async () => { root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button[data-action="interrupt"]')!.click(); });
    expect(region().querySelector('[role="alert"]')!.textContent).toContain('Agent Teams is not enabled');
  });

  it('keeps the inspector open when the agent leaves the roster', async () => {
    open('sub-1', [child]);
    await act(() => { render(<Inspector />, root); });
    await act(() => { state.value = { ...state.value, runs: state.value.runs.filter((r) => r.session_id !== 'sub-1') }; });
    expect(region().hidden).toBe(false);
    expect(region().textContent).toContain('Done');
    expect(root.querySelector('button[data-action="cancel"]')).toBeNull();
  });

  it('closes on Escape and gives focus back to the agent option', async () => {
    open('sub-1', [child]);
    const option = document.createElement('li');
    option.id = 'agent-sub-1';
    option.tabIndex = -1;
    document.body.append(option);
    const close = vi.spyOn(actions, 'inspect').mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    await act(() => { render(<Inspector />, root); });
    root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('#inspector-title')!.focus();
    await act(() => { region().dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Escape', bubbles: true })); });
    expect(close).toHaveBeenCalledWith(null);
    expect(document.activeElement).toBe(option);
    option.remove();
  });

  it('shows the main transcript for the main agent, with no extra actions', async () => {
    const s = reduce(reduce(initialState(), fx.snapshot), fx.message_user);
    state.value = inspect(s, 's1');
    await act(() => { render(<Inspector />, root); });
    expect(root.querySelector('#inspector-title')!.textContent).toBe('Coder');
    expect(region().querySelector('[role="log"]')!.textContent).toContain('Refactor the parser');
    expect(root.querySelector('button[data-action]')).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the inspector**

```tsx
import type { Ref } from 'preact';
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { agentTask, currentTool, findAgent, statusLabel, type Agent } from '../../state/agents';
import { actions, state } from '../../state/store';
import { Transcript } from '../log/Transcript';

const KIND = { main: 'Main agent', teammate: 'Teammate', subagent: 'Subagent' } as const;

function elapsed(ms: number): string {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.round(ms / 1000));
  return s < 60 ? `${s}s` : `${Math.floor(s / 60)}m ${String(s % 60).padStart(2, '0')}s`;
}

const fmtTokens = (n: number) => (n >= 1000 ? `${(n / 1000).toFixed(1)}k` : String(n));

export function Inspector({ panelRef }: { panelRef?: Ref<HTMLElement> } = {}) {
  const s = state.value;
  const ins = s.inspected;
  // The last agent seen for this selection. When the registry drops a
  // finished agent the inspector stays open on it, marked done.
  const known = useRef<Agent | null>(null);
  const found = ins ? findAgent(s, ins.sessionId) : null;
  if (found) known.current = found;
  else if (!ins) known.current = null;
  const agent = found ?? (known.current && ins && known.current.sessionId === ins.sessionId ? { ...known.current, status: 'done' as const } : null);
  const open = !!(ins && agent);

  const main = !!ins && ins.sessionId === s.sessionId;
  const messages = main ? s.messages : ins?.messages ?? [];
  const session = main ? s.session : ins?.session ?? null;
  const tool = currentTool(messages);
  const task = agent ? agentTask(s.team, agent) : null;
  const running = agent?.status === 'running' || agent?.status === 'provisioning';

  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now());
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!tool) return;
    setNow(Date.now());
    const timer = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now()), 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [tool?.name, tool?.since]);

  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState('');
  useEffect(() => {
    setText('');
    setError('');
    setNotice('');
  }, [ins?.sessionId]);

  const title = useRef<HTMLHeadingElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    // A pick in the Agents list keeps focus there, so the user can arrow
    // through agents; a pick anywhere else moves focus to the inspector.
    if (open && !(document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement && document.activeElement.closest('.agent-list'))) title.current?.focus();
  }, [ins?.sessionId, open]);

  const run = async (fn: () => Promise<string | null>, done: string) => {
    setError('');
    setNotice('');
    const err = await fn();
    if (err) setError(err);
    else setNotice(done);
  };

  const close = () => {
    const sid = ins?.sessionId;
    void actions.inspect(null);
    if (sid !== undefined) document.getElementById(`agent-${sid || 'main'}`)?.focus();
  };

  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key !== 'Escape' || e.isComposing) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    close();
  };

  return (
    <section ref={panelRef} class="panel panel--inspector glass scanlines" aria-labelledby="inspector-title" hidden={!open} onKeyDown={onKeyDown}>
      {open && agent && ins && (
        <>
          <header class="inspector__head">
            <span class="hud-label">{KIND[agent.kind]}</span>
            <h2 id="inspector-title" ref={title} tabIndex={-1}>{agent.name}</h2>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn--ghost inspector__close" aria-label="Close inspector" onClick={close}>×</button>
          </header>
          <dl class="inspector__facts">
            <div data-fact="status"><dt class="hud-label">Status</dt><dd data-status={agent.status}>{statusLabel(agent.status)}</dd></div>
            <div data-fact="tool"><dt class="hud-label">Current tool</dt><dd>{tool ? `${tool.name} · ${elapsed(now - tool.since)}` : 'None'}</dd></div>
            <div data-fact="model"><dt class="hud-label">Model</dt><dd>{agent.model ? `${agent.model}${agent.modelProvider ? ` · ${agent.modelProvider}` : ''}` : 'Not reported'}</dd></div>
            <div data-fact="usage"><dt class="hud-label">Tokens</dt><dd>{session ? `${fmtTokens(session.prompt_tokens + session.completion_tokens)} · $${session.cost.toFixed(2)}` : 'Not reported'}</dd></div>
            {task && <div data-fact="task"><dt class="hud-label">Task</dt><dd>{task.subject} <span class="hud-label">{task.status === 'in_progress' ? 'In progress' : 'Pending'}</span></dd></div>}
          </dl>
          {agent.kind !== 'main' && (
            <div class="inspector__actions">
              {agent.kind === 'teammate' && running && (
                <button type="button" class="btn btn--danger" data-action="interrupt" onClick={() => void run(() => actions.interruptTeammate(agent.member ?? agent.name), `Interrupted ${agent.name}`)}>Interrupt</button>
              )}
              {agent.kind === 'subagent' && running && (
                <button type="button" class="btn btn--danger" data-action="cancel" onClick={() => void run(() => actions.cancelAgent(agent.sessionId), `Cancelled ${agent.name}`)}>Cancel</button>
              )}
              {agent.kind === 'teammate' && agent.status !== 'done' && (
                <form class="inspector__message" onSubmit={(e) => {
                  e.preventDefault();
                  const body = text.trim();
                  if (!body) return;
                  void run(async () => {
                    const err = await actions.messageTeammate(agent.member ?? agent.name, body);
                    if (!err) setText('');
                    return err;
                  }, `Sent to ${agent.name}`);
                }}>
                  <label class="hud-label" for="inspector-message">Message {agent.name}</label>
                  <textarea id="inspector-message" rows={2} value={text} onInput={(e) => setText((e.currentTarget as HTMLTextAreaElement).value)} />
                  <button type="submit" class="btn btn--primary" data-action="message">Send</button>
                </form>
              )}
              {error && <p class="inspector__error" role="alert">{error}</p>}
              <p class="hud-label inspector__notice" role="status">{notice}</p>
            </div>
          )}
          {ins.loading
            ? <p class="hud-label">Loading the transcript</p>
            : <Transcript messages={messages} resetKey={ins.sessionId} label={`${agent.name} transcript`} empty={<p class="hud-label">No messages yet</p>} />}
        </>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}
```

The Send button carries `data-action="message"`; the main-agent test checks
`button[data-action]` is absent, which holds because the whole actions block
is skipped for `kind === 'main'`.

`AgentList.tsx` `onKeyDown` gains:

```ts
      case 'Escape':
        if (selected !== null) {
          e.preventDefault();
          void actions.inspect(null);
        }
        break;
```

`App.tsx`: add `const inspector = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);`, pass
`panels={[log, inspector]}` to `SceneLayer`, and inside `.cine-camera`
after the log section render `<Inspector panelRef={inspector} />`. The
section is always rendered (only `hidden` changes), so the cinematic flight
keeps a stable element to place.

`cinematic.ts`:

```ts
// One screen per panel: the log hangs left of the projector, the inspector
// right, mirrored, each turned toward the wide orbit.
export const PANEL_ANCHORS = [
  { x: -3.5, y: 1.8, z: 0.6, yaw: 0.55, scale: 0.008, faceCamera: false },
  { x: 3.5, y: 1.8, z: 0.6, yaw: -0.55, scale: 0.008, faceCamera: true },
];

export function anchorsFor(panels: HTMLElement[]): THREE.Object3D[] {
  return panels.map((_, i) => {
    const spec = PANEL_ANCHORS[Math.min(i, PANEL_ANCHORS.length - 1)];
    const anchor = new THREE.Object3D();
    anchor.position.set(spec.x, spec.y, spec.z);
    anchor.rotation.y = spec.yaw;
    anchor.scale.setScalar(spec.scale);
    anchor.userData.faceCamera = spec.faceCamera;
    anchor.updateMatrixWorld();
    return anchor;
  });
}
```

and in `write()`, before the panel loop:

```ts

    // picked stays readable from wherever they orbit to.

      if (!anchor.userData.faceCamera) continue;
      anchor.lookAt(camera.position.x, anchor.position.y, camera.position.z);
      anchor.updateMatrixWorld();
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`TaskBoard.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { TaskStatus, TeamTask } from '../../api/types';
import { scopedTasks } from '../../state/agents';
import { actions, state } from '../../state/store';

const LANES: Array<[TaskStatus, string]> = [['pending', 'Pending'], ['in_progress', 'In progress'], ['completed', 'Completed']];

export function TaskBoard() {
  const s = state.value;
  const tasks = scopedTasks(s.sessionId, s.team);
  if (tasks.length === 0) return null;
  const ownerSession = (t: TeamTask) => s.team.members.find((m) => m.id === t.owner_id)?.session_id;
  return (
    <section class="taskboard holo scanlines" aria-labelledby="taskboard-title">
      <h2 id="taskboard-title" class="hud-label">Team tasks</h2>
      <div class="taskboard__lanes">
        {LANES.map(([status, title]) => {
          const lane = tasks.filter((t) => t.status === status);
          return (
            <section key={status} class="taskboard__lane" data-status={status} aria-labelledby={`lane-${status}`}>
              <h3 id={`lane-${status}`} class="hud-label">{title} <span class="taskboard__count">{lane.length}</span></h3>
              <ul>
                {lane.map((t) => {
                  const sid = ownerSession(t);
                  return (
                    <li key={t.id} class="taskcard" data-ready={t.ready}>
                      <span class="taskcard__id hud-label">{t.id}</span>
                      <span class="taskcard__subject">{t.subject}</span>
                      {t.status === 'pending' && !t.ready && t.blocked_by.length > 0 && (
                        <span class="taskcard__blocked">Blocked by {t.blocked_by.join(', ')}</span>
                      )}
                      {t.owner_name && (sid
                        ? <button type="button" class="chip taskcard__owner" onClick={() => void actions.inspect(sid)}>{t.owner_name}</button>
                        : <span class="chip">{t.owner_name}</span>)}
                    </li>
                  );
                })}
              </ul>
            </section>
          );
        })}
      </div>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`TodoOrbit.tsx`:

```tsx
import { state } from '../../state/store';

const GLYPH: Record<string, string> = { completed: '✓', in_progress: '◐', pending: '○' };
const SPOKEN: Record<string, string> = { completed: 'done', in_progress: 'in progress', pending: 'to do' };
const R = 20;
const C = 2 * Math.PI * R;

export function TodoOrbit() {
  const todos = state.value.session?.todos ?? [];
  if (todos.length === 0) return null;
  const done = todos.filter((t) => t.status === 'completed').length;
  return (
    <section class="todo-orbit holo" aria-labelledby="todo-title">
      <h2 id="todo-title" class="hud-label">Todos <span>{done}/{todos.length}</span></h2>
      <svg class="todo-orbit__ring" viewBox="-24 -24 48 48" aria-hidden="true">
        <circle class="todo-orbit__track" r={R} />
        <circle class="todo-orbit__fill" r={R} stroke-dasharray={`${(C * done) / todos.length} ${C}`} transform="rotate(-90)" />
      </svg>
      <ol class="todo-orbit__list">
        {todos.map((t, i) => (
          <li key={i} data-status={t.status}>
            <span aria-hidden="true">{GLYPH[t.status] ?? '○'}</span>
            <span>{t.status === 'in_progress' && t.active_form ? t.active_form : t.content}</span>
            <span class="visually-hidden">, {SPOKEN[t.status] ?? t.status}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </section>
  );
}
```

`Stage.tsx` renders `<TodoOrbit />` and `<TaskBoard />` after the agent
list. `team.css`:

```css
.taskboard { position: absolute; inset-inline: var(--space-4); inset-block-end: var(--space-4); padding: var(--space-4); pointer-events: auto; max-block-size: 40%; overflow: auto; }
.taskboard__lanes { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr)); gap: var(--space-4); }
.taskboard__lane ul { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; display: grid; gap: 6px; }
.taskcard { display: grid; gap: 2px; padding: 6px 8px; border: 1px solid var(--edge); border-radius: var(--radius); background: var(--surface); }
.taskboard__lane[data-status='in_progress'] .taskcard { border-color: var(--cyan); }
.taskboard__lane[data-status='completed'] .taskcard { opacity: 0.7; }
.taskcard__blocked { color: var(--amber); font-size: var(--step--1); }
.taskcard__owner { justify-self: start; cursor: pointer; }
.todo-orbit { position: absolute; inset-inline-end: var(--space-4); inset-block-start: 18%; inline-size: min(16rem, 40%); padding: var(--space-4); pointer-events: auto; display: grid; grid-template-columns: 48px 1fr; gap: var(--space-1) var(--space-4); }
.todo-orbit h2 { grid-column: 1 / -1; margin: 0; }
.todo-orbit__ring { inline-size: 48px; block-size: 48px; }
.todo-orbit__track { fill: none; stroke: var(--edge); stroke-width: 3; }
.todo-orbit__fill { fill: none; stroke: var(--teal); stroke-width: 3; stroke-linecap: round; }
.todo-orbit__list { margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; display: grid; gap: 4px; font-size: var(--step--1); }
.todo-orbit__list li[data-status='completed'] { color: var(--text-muted); }
.todo-orbit__list li[data-status='in_progress'] { color: var(--cyan); }
@media (max-width: 1023px) { .taskboard__lanes { grid-template-columns: 1fr; } .todo-orbit { display: none; } }
```

the session's todos stay readable in the main agent's inspector transcript.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { FileSummary } from '../../api/types';
import { changeBlocks, fileGroups, relativePath } from './fileGroups';

const f = (path: string, over: Partial<FileSummary> = {}): FileSummary => ({

    expect(relativePath('/elsewhere/b.go', '/repo')).toBe('/elsewhere/b.go');
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement state and helpers**

`drawer.ts`:

```ts
import { signal } from '@preact/signals';
import { readStored, writeStored } from './storage';

export const drawerOpen = signal(false);
export const selectedFile = signal<string | null>(null);

export type DiffMode = 'split' | 'unified';
const KEY_DIFF = 'crush-web-diff-mode';
export const diffMode = signal<DiffMode>(readStored(KEY_DIFF) === 'unified' ? 'unified' : 'split');
export function setDiffMode(m: DiffMode): void {
  diffMode.value = m;
  writeStored(KEY_DIFF, m);
}

// Below 1024px the side panels share one slot and these pick which one
// shows (spec 3.3).
export type PanelTab = 'log' | 'agent' | 'files';
export const panelTab = signal<PanelTab>('log');

export function toggleDrawer(): void {
  drawerOpen.value = !drawerOpen.value;
  if (drawerOpen.value) panelTab.value = 'files';
}

export function openFile(path: string): void {
  drawerOpen.value = true;
  selectedFile.value = path;
  panelTab.value = 'files';
}
```

`fileGroups.ts`:

```ts
import type { FileSummary } from '../../api/types';

const slash = (p: string) => p.replace(/\\/g, '/');

export function relativePath(path: string, root: string): string {
  const p = slash(path);
  const r = slash(root).replace(/\/+$/, '');
  return r && p.startsWith(`${r}/`) ? p.slice(r.length + 1) : p;
}

export interface FileRow extends FileSummary { name: string; rel: string }

export function fileGroups(files: FileSummary[], root: string): Array<{ dir: string; files: FileRow[] }> {
  const groups = new Map<string, FileRow[]>();
  for (const f of files) {
    const rel = relativePath(f.path, root);
    const cut = rel.lastIndexOf('/');
    const dir = cut < 0 ? '' : rel.slice(0, cut);
    const row: FileRow = { ...f, rel, name: rel.slice(cut + 1) };
    groups.set(dir, [...(groups.get(dir) ?? []), row]);
  }
  return [...groups.entries()]
    .sort(([a], [b]) => a.localeCompare(b))
    .map(([dir, rows]) => ({ dir, files: rows.sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name)) }));
}

// changeBlocks draws the +/- bar: one block per ten changed lines up to the
// bar's length, split between additions and removals by their share.
export function changeBlocks(additions: number, removals: number, blocks = 5): Array<'add' | 'rem' | 'none'> {
  const total = additions + removals;
  const filled = total === 0 ? 0 : Math.min(blocks, Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / 10)));
  const add = total === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((filled * additions) / total);
  return Array.from({ length: blocks }, (_, i) => (i < add ? 'add' : i < filled ? 'rem' : 'none'));
}
```

Check `changeBlocks(30, 20)`: `filled = 5`, `add = round(3) = 3`, so three
add, two rem. `changeBlocks(1, 0)`: `filled = 1`, `add = 1`. Both match the
test.

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the drawer, the handle and the layout**

`FileDrawer.tsx`:

```tsx
import { drawerOpen, selectedFile } from '../../state/drawer';
import { state } from '../../state/store';
import { changeBlocks, fileGroups, relativePath } from './fileGroups';

export function FileDrawer() {
  const s = state.value;
  const open = drawerOpen.value;
  const selected = selectedFile.value;
  const groups = fileGroups(s.files, s.workingDir);
  return (
    <aside id="file-drawer" class="panel panel--drawer glass scanlines" aria-labelledby="drawer-title" data-open={open} inert={!open}>
      <header class="drawer__head">
        <h2 id="drawer-title">Edited files <span class="hud-label">{s.files.length}</span></h2>
        <button type="button" class="btn btn--ghost" aria-label="Close edited files" onClick={() => { drawerOpen.value = false; }}>×</button>
      </header>
      {s.files.length === 0 ? (
        <p class="drawer__empty">No edited files yet. Edits appear here as agents write.</p>
      ) : (
        <nav class="drawer__list" aria-label="Edited files">
          {groups.map((g) => (
            <section key={g.dir} class="drawer__group">
              <h3 class="hud-label">{g.dir || 'Project root'}</h3>
              <ul>
                {g.files.map((f) => (
                  <li key={f.path}>
                    <button type="button" class="file-row" aria-pressed={selected === f.path} title={f.rel} onClick={() => { selectedFile.value = f.path; }}>
                      <span class="file-row__name">{f.name}</span>
                      <span class="file-row__bars" aria-hidden="true">
                        {changeBlocks(f.additions, f.removals).map((b, i) => <span key={i} class="file-row__bar" data-kind={b} />)}
                      </span>
                      <span class="file-row__stat"><span class="file-row__add">+{f.additions}</span> <span class="file-row__rem">−{f.removals}</span></span>
                      {f.diagnostics.error > 0 && (
                        <span class="file-row__diag" data-level="error"><span aria-hidden="true">✕ </span>{f.diagnostics.error}<span class="visually-hidden"> {f.diagnostics.error === 1 ? 'error' : 'errors'}</span></span>
                      )}
                      {f.diagnostics.warning > 0 && (
                        <span class="file-row__diag" data-level="warning"><span aria-hidden="true">▲ </span>{f.diagnostics.warning}<span class="visually-hidden"> {f.diagnostics.warning === 1 ? 'warning' : 'warnings'}</span></span>
                      )}
                    </button>
                  </li>
                ))}
              </ul>
            </section>
          ))}
        </nav>
      )}
      <div class="drawer__diff">
        {selected && <p class="hud-label">{relativePath(selected, s.workingDir)}</p>}
      </div>
    </aside>
  );
}
```

The FileDrawer test expects the text `1 error`; the markup above produces
`1 error` through the visually hidden span (`textContent` includes it).

`PanelTabs.tsx` (shown only below 1024 px by CSS):

```tsx
import { drawerOpen, panelTab, type PanelTab } from '../state/drawer';
import { state } from '../state/store';

export function PanelTabs() {
  const agentOpen = state.value.inspected !== null;
  const pick = (t: PanelTab) => {
    panelTab.value = t;
    if (t === 'files') drawerOpen.value = true;
  };
  const tab = (t: PanelTab, label: string, disabled = false) => (
    <button type="button" class="hud__mode-btn" aria-pressed={panelTab.value === t} disabled={disabled} onClick={() => pick(t)}>{label}</button>
  );
  return (
    <div class="panel-tabs" role="group" aria-label="Panel">
      {tab('log', 'Log')}
      {tab('agent', 'Agent', !agentOpen)}
      {tab('files', 'Files')}
    </div>
  );
}
```

```tsx
      </button>
```

```css
.panel--drawer { display: flex; flex-direction: column; min-block-size: 0; transition: transform var(--dur-in) var(--ease-out), opacity var(--dur-in) var(--ease-out); }
.holodeck__grid[data-drawer='closed'] .panel--drawer {

}

.drawer__head h2 { margin: 0; font: 600 var(--step-1) var(--font-hud); }
.drawer__empty { padding: 0 var(--space-4); color: var(--text-muted); }

.drawer__group ul { list-style: none; margin: 0 0 var(--space-4); padding: 0; display: grid; gap: 2px; }

.file-row[aria-pressed='true'] { border-color: var(--cyan); background: var(--cyan-dim); }

.drawer__diff { flex: 1; min-block-size: 0; display: flex; flex-direction: column; padding: 0 var(--space-4) var(--space-4); }
.drawer-handle { position: absolute; inset-block-start: var(--space-4); inset-inline-end: var(--space-4); pointer-events: auto; padding: 6px 12px; font: 500 var(--step--1) var(--font-hud); cursor: pointer; }
[data-motion='off'] .panel--drawer, [data-motion='reduced'] .panel--drawer { transition-duration: 1ms; }
```

Removals use amber, not magenta: spec 3.2 reserves magenta for "needs you"

with `var(--amber)`. The diff view (Task 18) uses the same pair.

### Task 18: Diff view (CodeMirror 6 merge view, lazy) and the lazy-chunk guard

Spec 3.8 ("selecting a file opens a CodeMirror 6 merge view (side-by-side or
unified, read-only, syntax highlighted), a version stepper ... CodeMirror is
loaded lazily"), 3.10 (separate chunk), 4 (over 1 MB falls back to unified
text), 6 (`@codemirror/merge`).

CSP finding that shapes this task: CodeMirror's `style-mod`
(`node_modules/style-mod/src/style-mod.js`, `StyleSet` constructor) injects
a `<style>` element whenever the root is a document (`root.head` exists),
and `style-src 'self'` blocks that, which would leave the editor unstyled
and the CSP violation log full. Inside a shadow root (no `head`) it uses
`adoptedStyleSheets`, which the CSP allows. So the editor mounts in a
shadow root, and our diff styles are adopted there the same way. Every
inline style CodeMirror writes goes through the CSSOM (`style.cssText`,
`style.height`), which the CSP allows.

**Files:**
- Modify: `web/package.json`, `web/package-lock.json` (new dependencies)
- Create: `web/src/ui/files/DiffPanel.tsx`, `DiffPanel.test.tsx`
- Create: `web/src/ui/files/diff/codemirror.ts`, `web/src/ui/files/diff/languages.ts`, `languages.test.ts`
- Create: `web/src/styles/diff.css` (adopted inside the shadow root; added to `EXPECTED_SHEETS`, not imported by `main.tsx`)
- Modify: `web/src/ui/files/FileDrawer.tsx` (render `DiffPanel` in `.drawer__diff`)
- Modify: `web/scripts/budget.mjs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `api.diff` (Task 10), `diffMode`, `setDiffMode`, `selectedFile` (Task 17), `relativePath` (Task 17), `findAgent` (Task 10).
- Produces:
  - `mountDiff(host: HTMLElement, d: FileDiff, mode: DiffMode, label: string): Promise<() => void>` in the lazy chunk
  - `languageExtension(name: string): Promise<Extension>` and `LANGUAGES: string[]` (exactly the names `languageFor` in `internal/web/files.go` returns, minus `text`)
  - `DiffPanel({ path })`: `section` labelled by the relative path; selects `name="from"` and `name="to"`; layout buttons `data-mode="split"` ("Side by side") and `data-mode="unified"`; "Edited by" chips that inspect the agents in `session_ids`; a `pre.diff-unified` fallback with `data-kind` of `add`, `rem`, `hunk`, `ctx` per line
  - `npm run budget` also fails when an eager JavaScript file contains `isWebGLRenderer` (three.js) or `cm-editor` (CodeMirror)

```tsx
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { render } from 'preact';
import { act } from 'preact/test-utils';
import fixtures from '../../state/__fixtures__/events.json';
import { ApiError, api } from '../../api/http';
import type { FileDiff, Frame } from '../../api/types';
import { diffMode } from '../../state/drawer';
import { initialState, reduce } from '../../state/reducer';
import { actions, state } from '../../state/store';

const mounted = vi.hoisted(() => ({ modes: [] as string[], destroyed: 0 }));
vi.mock('./diff/codemirror', () => ({
  mountDiff: async (_host: HTMLElement, _d: unknown, mode: string) => {
    mounted.modes.push(mode);
    return () => { mounted.destroyed++; };
  },
}));
import { DiffPanel } from './DiffPanel';

const fx = fixtures as unknown as Record<string, Frame>;
const root = document.createElement('div');
const diff = (over: Partial<FileDiff> = {}): FileDiff => ({
  path: '/repo/parser.go', from: 0, to: 1, old: 'a\n', new: 'a\nb\n',
  unified: '--- a/repo/parser.go\n+++ b/repo/parser.go\n@@ -1 +1,2 @@\n a\n+b\n', language: 'go', truncated: false, ...over,
});
const flush = () => act(async () => { await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0)); });

beforeEach(() => {
  state.value = reduce(initialState(), fx.snapshot);
  mounted.modes = [];
  mounted.destroyed = 0;
  diffMode.value = 'split';
});
afterEach(async () => {
  await act(() => { render(null, root); });
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
});

describe('DiffPanel', () => {
  it('loads the diff and mounts the editor lazily in the chosen layout', async () => {
    const get = vi.spyOn(api, 'diff').mockResolvedValue(diff());
    await act(() => { render(<DiffPanel path="/repo/parser.go" />, root); });
    await flush();
    expect(get).toHaveBeenCalledWith('s1', '/repo/parser.go', undefined, undefined);
    expect(mounted.modes).toEqual(['split']);
    await act(() => { root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button[data-mode="unified"]')!.click(); });
    await flush();
    expect(mounted.modes).toEqual(['split', 'unified']);
    expect(mounted.destroyed).toBe(1);
  });

  it('renders the unified fallback when the diff is truncated', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(api, 'diff').mockResolvedValue(diff({ old: '', new: '', truncated: true }));
    await act(() => { render(<DiffPanel path="/repo/parser.go" />, root); });
    await flush();
    expect(mounted.modes).toEqual([]);
    expect(root.textContent).toContain('over 1 MB');
    expect(root.querySelector('.diff-unified [data-kind="add"]')!.textContent).toContain('+b');
    expect(root.querySelector('.diff-unified [data-kind="hunk"]')).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it('asks for the versions the stepper picks', async () => {
    const get = vi.spyOn(api, 'diff').mockResolvedValue(diff());
    await act(() => { render(<DiffPanel path="/repo/parser.go" />, root); });
    await flush();
    const from = root.querySelector<HTMLSelectElement>('select[name="from"]')!;
    expect([...from.options].map((o) => o.value)).toEqual(['0', '1']);
    await act(() => {
      from.value = '1';
      from.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
    });
    await flush();
    expect(get).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith('s1', '/repo/parser.go', 1, 1);
  });

  it('shows the gateway error with a retry', async () => {
    const get = vi.spyOn(api, 'diff').mockRejectedValue(new ApiError(404, 'unknown_path', 'the session has no history for this path'));
    await act(() => { render(<DiffPanel path="/repo/parser.go" />, root); });
    await flush();
    expect(root.querySelector('[role="alert"]')!.textContent).toContain('the session has no history for this path');
    await act(() => { root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[role="alert"] button')!.click(); });
    await flush();
    expect(get).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it('links the agents that edited the file', async () => {
    vi.spyOn(api, 'diff').mockResolvedValue(diff());
    const inspect = vi.spyOn(actions, 'inspect').mockResolvedValue(undefined);
    await act(() => { render(<DiffPanel path="/repo/parser.go" />, root); });
    await flush();
    const chips = [...root.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('.diffpanel__editors button')];
    expect(chips.map((c) => c.textContent)).toEqual(['Coder', 'Map the parser']);
    await act(() => { chips[1].click(); });
    expect(inspect).toHaveBeenCalledWith('sub-1');
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the lazy chunk**

`languages.ts`:

```ts
import type { Extension } from '@codemirror/state';

// One dynamic import per language, so a Go diff never downloads the HTML
// grammar. The names match languageFor in internal/web/files.go.
const LOADERS: Record<string, () => Promise<Extension>> = {
  go: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-go')).go(),
  typescript: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-javascript')).javascript({ typescript: true }),
  tsx: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-javascript')).javascript({ typescript: true, jsx: true }),
  javascript: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-javascript')).javascript(),
  jsx: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-javascript')).javascript({ jsx: true }),
  json: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-json')).json(),
  css: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-css')).css(),
  html: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-html')).html(),
  markdown: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-markdown')).markdown(),
  python: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-python')).python(),
  yaml: async () => (await import('@codemirror/lang-yaml')).yaml(),
};

export const LANGUAGES = Object.keys(LOADERS);

export async function languageExtension(name: string): Promise<Extension> {
  const load = LOADERS[name];
  if (!load) return [];
  try {
    return await load();
  } catch {
    // A grammar that fails to load leaves the diff readable as plain text.
    return [];
  }
}
```

`codemirror.ts`:

```ts
import { MergeView, unifiedMergeView } from '@codemirror/merge';
import { syntaxHighlighting } from '@codemirror/language';
import { EditorState, type Extension } from '@codemirror/state';
import { EditorView, lineNumbers } from '@codemirror/view';
import { classHighlighter } from '@lezer/highlight';
import type { FileDiff } from '../../../api/types';
import type { DiffMode } from '../../../state/drawer';
import diffCss from '../../../styles/diff.css?inline';
import { languageExtension } from './languages';

let sheet: CSSStyleSheet | null = null;

// style-mod writes a <style> element into a document root, which the CSP
// blocks, and adopts a constructed sheet into a shadow root, which it
// allows. The editor therefore lives in a shadow root, with our own diff
// styles adopted next to CodeMirror's.
function shadowFor(host: HTMLElement): ShadowRoot {
  const root = host.shadowRoot ?? host.attachShadow({ mode: 'open' });
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = new CSSStyleSheet();
    sheet.replaceSync(diffCss);
  }
  if (!root.adoptedStyleSheets.includes(sheet)) root.adoptedStyleSheets = [...root.adoptedStyleSheets, sheet];
  return root;
}

const COLLAPSE = { margin: 3, minSize: 6 };

export async function mountDiff(host: HTMLElement, d: FileDiff, mode: DiffMode, label: string): Promise<() => void> {
  const root = shadowFor(host);
  root.replaceChildren();
  const parent = document.createElement('div');
  parent.className = 'diff';
  root.append(parent);
  const base: Extension[] = [
    EditorState.readOnly.of(true),
    EditorView.editable.of(false),
    lineNumbers(),
    EditorView.lineWrapping,
    syntaxHighlighting(classHighlighter),
    await languageExtension(d.language),
  ];
  const named = (text: string) => EditorView.contentAttributes.of({ 'aria-label': text });
  if (mode === 'split') {
    const view = new MergeView({
      a: { doc: d.old, extensions: [...base, named(`${label}, version ${d.from}`)] },
      b: { doc: d.new, extensions: [...base, named(`${label}, version ${d.to}`)] },
      parent,
      root,
      gutter: true,
      collapseUnchanged: COLLAPSE,
    });
    return () => view.destroy();
  }
  const view = new EditorView({
    doc: d.new,
    parent,
    root,
    extensions: [
      ...base,
      unifiedMergeView({ original: d.old, mergeControls: false, gutter: true, collapseUnchanged: COLLAPSE }),
      named(`${label}, version ${d.from} to ${d.to}`),
    ],
  });
  return () => view.destroy();
}
```
