- [ ] **Step 5: Implement `DiffPanel` and wire it into the drawer**

```tsx
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'preact/hooks';
import { ApiError, api } from '../../api/http';
import type { FileDiff } from '../../api/types';
import { findAgent, type Agent } from '../../state/agents';
import { diffMode, setDiffMode, type DiffMode } from '../../state/drawer';
import { actions, state } from '../../state/store';
import { relativePath } from './fileGroups';

function lineKind(line: string): 'add' | 'rem' | 'hunk' | 'ctx' {
  if (line.startsWith('@@')) return 'hunk';
  if (line.startsWith('+') && !line.startsWith('+++')) return 'add';
  if (line.startsWith('-') && !line.startsWith('---')) return 'rem';
  return 'ctx';
}

function UnifiedText({ text }: { text: string }) {
  // tabIndex lets a keyboard user scroll the long fallback.
  return (
    <pre class="diff-unified" tabIndex={0}>
      {text.split('\n').map((line, i) => <span key={i} data-kind={lineKind(line)}>{line}{'\n'}</span>)}
    </pre>
  );
}

const LAYOUTS: Array<[DiffMode, string]> = [['split', 'Side by side'], ['unified', 'Unified']];

export function DiffPanel({ path }: { path: string }) {
  const s = state.value;
  const file = s.files.find((f) => f.path === path);
  const versions = file?.version_ids ?? [];
  const latest = versions.at(-1);
  const [range, setRange] = useState<{ from?: number; to?: number }>({});
  const [diff, setDiff] = useState<FileDiff | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [attempt, setAttempt] = useState(0);
  const host = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const mode = diffMode.value;
  const rel = relativePath(path, s.workingDir);
  const sid = s.sessionId;

  useEffect(() => {
    setRange({});
    setDiff(null);
  }, [path]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!sid) return;
    let live = true;
    setError('');
    api.diff(sid, path, range.from, range.to)
      .then((d) => { if (live) setDiff(d); })
      .catch((err) => { if (live) setError(err instanceof ApiError ? err.message : 'The diff did not load'); });
    return () => { live = false; };
  }, [sid, path, range.from, range.to, latest, attempt]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const el = host.current;
    if (!el || !diff || diff.truncated) return;
    let live = true;
    let destroy: (() => void) | null = null;
    import('./diff/codemirror')
      .then(({ mountDiff }) => mountDiff(el, diff, mode, rel))
      .then((stop) => { if (live) destroy = stop; else stop(); })
      .catch(() => { if (live) setError('The diff view could not load'); });
    return () => {
      live = false;
      destroy?.();
    };
  }, [diff, mode]);

  const pick = (key: 'from' | 'to') => (e: Event) => {
    const v = Number((e.currentTarget as HTMLSelectElement).value);
    setRange({ from: diff?.from, to: diff?.to, [key]: v });
  };
  const editors = (file?.session_ids ?? []).map((id) => findAgent(s, id)).filter((a): a is Agent => a !== null);

  return (
    <section class="diffpanel" aria-labelledby="diff-title">
      <header class="diffpanel__head">
        <h3 id="diff-title" class="diffpanel__title">{rel}</h3>
        <label class="hud-label">From{' '}
          <select class="hud__select" name="from" value={String(diff?.from ?? '')} disabled={!diff} onChange={pick('from')}>
            {versions.map((v) => <option key={v} value={String(v)}>Version {v}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
        <label class="hud-label">To{' '}
          <select class="hud__select" name="to" value={String(diff?.to ?? '')} disabled={!diff} onChange={pick('to')}>
            {versions.map((v) => <option key={v} value={String(v)}>Version {v}</option>)}
          </select>
        </label>
        <div class="hud__mode" role="group" aria-label="Diff layout">
          {LAYOUTS.map(([m, label]) => (
            <button key={m} type="button" class="hud__mode-btn" data-mode={m} data-active={mode === m} aria-pressed={mode === m} onClick={() => setDiffMode(m)}>{label}</button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </header>
      {editors.length > 0 && (
        <p class="diffpanel__editors hud-label">Edited by{' '}
          {editors.map((a) => <button key={a.sessionId} type="button" class="chip" onClick={() => void actions.inspect(a.sessionId)}>{a.name}</button>)}
        </p>
      )}
      {error && (
        <p class="diffpanel__error" role="alert">{error}{' '}
          <button type="button" class="link-button" onClick={() => setAttempt((n) => n + 1)}>Retry</button>
        </p>
      )}
      {!diff && !error && <p class="hud-label">Loading the diff</p>}
      {diff?.truncated
        ? <><p class="hud-label">This diff is over 1 MB, so only the unified text is shown.</p><UnifiedText text={diff.unified} /></>
        : <div class="diffpanel__view" ref={host} />}
    </section>
  );
}
```

In `FileDrawer.tsx`, `.drawer__diff` renders
`{selected && <DiffPanel key={selected} path={selected} />}` instead of the
path label. `drawer.css` gains:

```css
.diffpanel { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: var(--space-1); min-block-size: 0; flex: 1; }
.diffpanel__head { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; align-items: center; gap: var(--space-1) var(--space-4); }
.diffpanel__title { margin: 0; font: 500 var(--step-0) var(--font-code); overflow-wrap: anywhere; flex: 1 1 100%; }
.diffpanel__view { flex: 1; min-block-size: 18rem; }
.diffpanel__error { color: var(--magenta); }
.diff-unified { overflow: auto; margin: 0; padding: var(--space-1); background: var(--void-deep); border: 1px solid var(--edge); border-radius: var(--radius); font: 400 var(--step--1)/1.5 var(--font-code); flex: 1; min-block-size: 0; }
.diff-unified [data-kind='add'] { color: var(--teal); }
```

- [ ] **Step 6: The lazy-chunk guard in `budget.mjs`**

After the total, add:

```js
// Heavy libraries must stay in lazy chunks. These strings survive
// minification and appear only in their code, so finding one in an eager
// file means a static import slipped in somewhere.
const LAZY_MARKERS = [['three.js', 'isWebGLRenderer'], ['CodeMirror', 'cm-editor']];
for (const ref of refs) {
  if (!ref.endsWith('.js')) continue;
  const text = readFileSync(path.join(dist, ref.replace(/^\//, '')), 'utf8');
  for (const [lib, marker] of LAZY_MARKERS) {
    if (text.includes(marker)) {
      console.error(`${ref} pulls ${lib} into the eager bundle (found ${marker})`);
      process.exitCode = 1;
    }
  }
}
```

```tsx
  it('a late cancel of an old batch does not unlock the next batch', async () => {
    let finishCancel!: (v: string | null) => void;
    vi.spyOn(actions, 'cancelQuestion').mockImplementation(() => new Promise((r) => { finishCancel = r; }));
    const answer = vi.spyOn(actions, 'answer').mockImplementation(() => new Promise(() => {}));
    seed({ question: batch() });
    await mount();
    await act(() => { window.dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent('keydown', { key: 'Escape' })); });
    await act(() => { state.value = { ...state.value, question: { ...freeText(), id: 'b9' } }; });
    const text = root.querySelector<HTMLTextAreaElement>('textarea')!;
    await act(() => { text.value = 'notes'; text.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true })); });
    await act(() => { root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button[type="submit"]')!.click(); });
    await act(async () => { finishCancel(null); });
    await act(() => { root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button[type="submit"]')!.click(); });
    expect(answer).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
```

with the helper (next to the file's existing `batch()`):

`palette.test.ts`:

```ts
import { readdirSync, readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';

// The M1 palette before the neon redesign. Stylesheets reach colors
// through the tokens (or color-mix over them), never through these.
const RETIRED = ['0.82 0.14 210', '0.68 0.24 330', '0.60 0.20 290', '0.80 0.15 75'];
const DIR = '.';

describe('palette', () => {
  it('keeps retired colors out of every stylesheet', () => {
    const dir = fileURLToPath(new URL(DIR, import.meta.url));
    for (const sheet of readdirSync(dir).filter((f) => f.endsWith('.css'))) {
      const css = readFileSync(path.join(dir, sheet), 'utf8');
      for (const value of RETIRED) expect(css, `${sheet} keeps ${value}`).not.toContain(value);
    }
  });
});
```

`CineHint.test.tsx` (a unit of its own: in `App` the mocked scene fails and
cinematic mode closes at once):

```tsx
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { render } from 'preact';
import { act } from 'preact/test-utils';
import { setCinematic } from '../state/cinematic';
import { resetWebglProbe, sceneFailed } from '../state/effects';
import { lowPower } from '../state/motion';
import { CineHint } from './CineHint';

describe('CineHint', () => {
  it('shows the orbit keys while in cinematic mode', async () => {
    const original = HTMLCanvasElement.prototype.getContext;
    // A context object is enough for the probe, so cinematic mode is allowed.
    HTMLCanvasElement.prototype.getContext = (() => ({ getExtension: () => null })) as unknown as typeof original;
    resetWebglProbe();
    lowPower.value = false;
    sceneFailed.value = false;
    const root = document.createElement('div');
    try {
      await act(() => { render(<CineHint />, root); });
      expect(root.querySelector('.cine-hint')).toBeNull();
      await act(() => { setCinematic(true); });
      expect(root.querySelector('.cine-hint')!.textContent).toContain('Shift + arrow keys');
      await act(() => { setCinematic(false); });
      expect(root.querySelector('.cine-hint')).toBeNull();
    } finally {
      await act(() => { render(null, root); });
      HTMLCanvasElement.prototype.getContext = original;
      resetWebglProbe();
    }
  });
});
```

`MotionMenu.tsx`:

```tsx
  const menu = useRef<HTMLDetailsElement>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    const onDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      const el = menu.current;
      if (el?.open && !el.contains(e.target as Node)) el.open = false;
    };
    document.addEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
    return () => document.removeEventListener('pointerdown', onDown);
  }, []);
  const onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key !== 'Escape' || !menu.current?.open) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    menu.current.open = false;
    summary.current?.focus();
  };
```

with `<details ref={menu} class="motion-menu" onKeyDown={onKeyDown}>`.

  (used by `addFiles`) and

  `} catch { setError('The prompt did not reach crush.'); } finally {`.

  (`setCaret((e.currentTarget as HTMLTextAreaElement).selectionStart)`), and

dialogs are `alertdialog` and `dialog` with focus moved into them, which
announces them; errors inside stay `role="alert"`).

`QuestionForm.tsx`:
- The form's `onSubmit` becomes
  `onSubmit={(e) => { e.preventDefault(); if (multi && tab !== confirmTab) { setTab(tab + 1); return; } void submit(); }}`.
- `submit` and `cancel` capture `const id = req.id;` at their start, and
  their `finally` blocks become
  `finally { if (lastId.current === id) { busy.current = false; setSending(false); } }`.

`reducer.ts`:

```ts
// unmarkAnswered undoes markAnswered after a request that never reached
// the workspace, so the terminal's answer still gets its toast.
export function unmarkAnswered(s: State, id: string): State {
  return s.answered.includes(id) ? { ...s, answered: s.answered.filter((x) => x !== id) } : s;
}
```

`store.ts`: in `grant`, `answer` and `cancelQuestion`, the final
`return errorText(err);` becomes:

```ts
      dispatch((s) => unmarkAnswered(s, id));
      return errorText(err);
```

(`batchId` in `answer` and `cancelQuestion`). Where a call resolves with a
non-null error string instead of throwing, apply the same before returning.

`oklch(0.60 0.20 290 / 0.12)` becomes `color-mix(in oklch, var(--violet) 12%, transparent)`,
`oklch(0.80 0.15 75 / 0.08)` becomes `color-mix(in oklch, var(--amber) 8%, transparent)`.
`grep -n "oklch(" web/src/styles/*.css | grep -v tokens.css` lists them
(deck.css, interrupt.css, log.css, shell.css).

```tsx
import { cinematic } from '../state/cinematic';

// aria-hidden: the focused canvas already carries the same words in its
// label, so this is for sighted keyboard users only.
export function CineHint() {

  return <p class="cine-hint holo hud-label" aria-hidden="true">Drag or use Shift + arrow keys to orbit. Esc leaves.</p>;
}
```

```ts
import { expect, test } from './fixture';

test('selects an agent with the keyboard and reads what it is doing', async ({ page, gateway }) => {
  await page.goto(gateway.launch);
  await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'Linked' })).toBeVisible();
  const list = page.getByRole('listbox', { name: 'Agents' });
  await expect(list.getByRole('option')).toHaveCount(3);
  await list.getByRole('option', { name: /Coder/ }).focus();
  await page.keyboard.press('ArrowDown');
  await expect(list.getByRole('option', { name: /Map the parser/ })).toBeFocused();
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  const inspector = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Map the parser' });
  await expect(inspector).toBeVisible();
  await expect(inspector).toContainText('grep');
  await expect(inspector.getByRole('log')).toContainText('Scanning the parser package');
  await page.keyboard.press('Escape');
  await expect(inspector).toBeHidden();
  await expect(list.getByRole('option', { name: /Map the parser/ })).toBeFocused();
});

test('selects an agent node with the pointer and messages it', async ({ page, gateway }) => {
  await page.goto(gateway.launch);
  await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'Linked' })).toBeVisible();
  const node = page.locator('[data-agent-node="team-m-1"]');
  await expect(node).toHaveAttribute('data-visible', 'true', { timeout: 15_000 });
  await node.click();
  const inspector = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'alice' });
  await expect(inspector).toContainText('Port the parser');
  await inspector.getByLabel('Message alice').fill('rebase first');
  await inspector.getByRole('button', { name: 'Send' }).click();
  await expect(inspector.getByRole('status')).toContainText('Sent to alice');
});

test('selects a 2D node in low power mode', async ({ page, gateway }) => {
  await page.addInitScript(() => localStorage.setItem('crush-web-low-power', '1'));
  await page.goto(gateway.launch);
  await expect(page.locator('svg.constellation-svg')).toBeVisible();
  await page.locator('svg [data-agent-node="sub-1"]').click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Map the parser' })).toBeVisible();
});

test('delegated work streams into the inspector and the drawer', async ({ page, gateway }) => {
  await page.goto(gateway.launch);
  await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'Linked' })).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('listbox', { name: 'Agents' }).getByRole('option', { name: /Map the parser/ }).click();
  await page.getByLabel('Transmit to crush').fill('delegate the tokenizer');
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  const inspector = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Map the parser' });
  await expect(inspector.getByRole('log')).toContainText('Rewriting the tokenizer loop.');
  await expect(inspector).toContainText('edit');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Files/ }).click();
  await page.getByRole('complementary', { name: /Edited files/ }).getByRole('button', { name: /parser\.go/ }).click();
  await expect(page.locator('.cm-mergeView')).toContainText('func tokenize');
});
```

`files.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { expect, test } from './fixture';

test('opens a diff from the file drawer without a CSP violation', async ({ page, gateway }) => {
  await page.addInitScript(() => {
    const violations: string[] = [];
    Object.assign(window, { __cspViolations: violations });
    window.addEventListener('securitypolicyviolation', (e) => violations.push(`${e.violatedDirective} ${e.blockedURI}`));
  });
  await page.goto(gateway.launch);
  await expect(page.getByRole('status').filter({ hasText: 'Linked' })).toBeVisible();
  const handle = page.getByRole('button', { name: /^Files/ });
  await expect(handle).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
  await handle.click();
  await expect(handle).toHaveAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
  const drawer = page.getByRole('complementary', { name: /Edited files/ });
  await expect(drawer).toContainText('app.ts');
  await drawer.getByRole('button', { name: /parser\.go/ }).click();
  const diff = drawer.getByRole('region', { name: /parser\.go/ });
  await expect(diff.locator('.cm-mergeView')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(diff.locator('.cm-mergeView')).toContainText('func Parse(src string) []Token');
  await diff.getByRole('button', { name: 'Unified' }).click();
  await expect(diff.locator('.cm-mergeView')).toHaveCount(0);
  await expect(diff.locator('.cm-editor')).toHaveCount(1);
  await diff.getByLabel('From').selectOption('1');
  await expect(diff.locator('.cm-editor')).not.toContainText('func Parse() {}');
  expect(await page.evaluate(() => (window as unknown as { __cspViolations: string[] }).__cspViolations)).toEqual([]);
});
```

## Deviations from the spec (deliberate)

- `team_state` carries a trimmed `TeamState` (roster, live tasks with owner
  names and readiness, snake_case) instead of the raw `agentteam.State`: the
  inbox can hold 64 KiB messages the browser never shows, and the ring and
  snapshots would carry them for every tab.
- Inactive teammates stay on the roster, dimmed; the TUI agents dialog lists
  only running and provisioning members. Spec 3.4 gives inactive its own
  node look, which needs the node.
- Teammate messages are sent as the Lead. The Team service has no "user"
  sender, and a teammate treats peer messages by sender name.
- Spec 3.8 asks for links from each version to the tool call that produced
  it. `history.File` records no tool call, so the diff panel links the
  agents (sessions) that edited the file instead; the per-version link
  needs a history schema change and is left for a later milestone.
- The Chronoline (spec 3.8, tagged M2) is not in this plan: the M2 parity
  rows in spec 5 do not list it and this plan's scope came from those rows.
  It needs a lead ruling (M2 follow-up or M3).
- Removals are amber, not red or magenta (spec 3.2 reserves magenta).
- Below 1024 px the side panels switch with a "Log / Agent / Files" toggle
  group (`aria-pressed` buttons), not ARIA tabs: the panels are landmarks
  that keep their own roles in the wide layout.
- The todo orbit hides below 1024 px, where the stage is a 220 px strip.

## Deferred items not taken

- G1-4 AppWorkspace bridge blocks on a stalled consumer: blocking hands the overflow to the broker's 4096-event buffer, which already drops with a throttled warning; dropping at the bridge's 256 would lose events sooner (Task 3 documents this).
- G1-9 API `Cache-Control: no-store`: done in the M1 final fix wave.
- G1-10 (part) case-sensitive Host compare: fails closed and browsers send the lowercase host.
- G1-13 pump reads `CurrentSessionID` late: fixed in the M1 final fix wave (`Start` reads it).
- G1-16 (part) weak "See above." nolint wording: cosmetic.
- G1-17 browser runs detached with no tracking: runs belong to the workspace like TUI runs; the gateway only stops when crush exits, which cancels them anyway.
- G1-19 no attachment count cap: the 32 MiB body cap already bounds memory on a single-user loopback tab.
- G1-21 OpenBrowser warning, `--web=false` precedence test, parked opener goroutine: CLI paths M2 does not touch; the warning is unreachable in practice and the goroutine ends with the process.
- G1-22 manual TTY check of `/web`: user-owned manual check (I4).
- G1-23 `openWeb` empty-URL branch and two TUI test nits: the branch no longer exists; the nits do not change behavior.
- G1-24 `TestClientServerSpawnRace` flake: pre-existing, outside web code, not reproduced.
- G1-25 (part) all fixture frames carry seq 1: fixtures are applied one at a time, never as a sequence.
- G1-26 every font subset bundled: `unicode-range` means browsers fetch only needed subsets; trimming would drop glyphs for non-Latin text; the cost is binary size only.
- G1-27 `tokens.test.ts` duplicates token values: deliberate, it pins the spec values verbatim.
- G1-28 live at open, flip per open-close cycle, probe timer on stop: live at open is deliberate (a resume at the head receives no frame, documented in `stream.ts`); the timer is harmless.
- G1-29 (part) extra grid gap, favicon never restored, hue test, `inert` before dialogs: the grid areas rewrite (Task 13) replaces the rows; the tab dies with its favicon; the hue collision is theoretical; `inert` landed in M1 Task 15.
- G1-31 (part) `actions.answer` without `not_pending`, untested contenteditable branch: the former landed in M1; the app has no contenteditable element.
- G1-32 (part) `.hud` isolation, `web.yml` concurrency, timeout and paths, token in webfake's dev hint: the first two are done (HUD stacking context, workflow settings); the hint exists to hand the token to a developer and the fixture pipes and redacts stderr.
- G1-34 (part) `aria-activedescendant` and `aria-expanded` on the textarea: ARIA in HTML allows neither `combobox` nor `aria-expanded` on a textarea; a real fix is an input-based combobox, which belongs with the M3 palette.
- G1-36 `mountConstellation` lifecycle, `token()` misparse, hex comments, gamut clamp: `constellation.ts` was deleted in the redesign; every token is oklch (pinned by `tokens.test.ts`); the hue shift is cosmetic.
- G1-37 (part) `boot()` guard, `store.ts`/`motion.ts` untested: `main.tsx` calls `boot` once (commented); the store has a suite now and motion storage is covered by Task 11.
- G1-38 `TestSessionFollowPublished` flake: fixed with G1-13 in the M1 fix wave.
- G1-39 (part) open seam mutated after construction, undocumented client/server support: tests set the seam before any call; user docs for web mode are M4 work.
- G1-40 startup update message TTL: TUI status bar behavior, outside web mode.
- G2-1 FogExp2 old palette in `constellation.ts`: file deleted.
- G2-3 `Set` cast in telemetry: cosmetic and safe.
- G2-4 fragile `tools.length` early return: safe today because `seen` gates it; accepted by the reviewer.
- G2-5 `seen` never pruned: bounded by the session's tool calls and reset on a session switch.
- G2-7 toast offsets: done (the HUD publishes `--hud-h`).
- G2-8 brackets scrolling inside `.interrupt`: fixed by "scroll the interrupt dialog inside its frame".
- G2-9 `stageRect` without a consumer: obsolete, the scene reads it as its focus.
- G2-11 reduced-motion test checks the fade-in only: the off-motion cinematic smoke test waits on the cross-fade end.
- G2-12 SceneLayer disposed guard untested: done (`SceneLayer.unmount.test.tsx`).
- G2-13 world `dispose()` untested: done (`world.test.ts`).
- G2-16 floor shader and beams only checked in a deleted harness: confirmed by the committed visual baselines.
- G2-17 flash tint compounding over a few frames: cosmetic, reduced motion only.
- G2-18 `setBoot`, dispose and error flicker untested: covered since (`core.test.ts` dispose and flicker, the mount boot tests).
- G2-19 `coreLook` returns the shared table: nothing mutates it.
- G2-20 cap test does not check the oldest ring: done ("recycles the oldest ring past the cap").
- G2-21 bursts dispose and opacity falloff untested: done (`bursts.test.ts`).
- G2-22 "colour" spelling, meshes left in the group: done (`group.clear()`, spelling fixed).
- G2-23 renderer stranded on a failed construction: done (`mountScene` try and catch).
- G2-24 `mount.ts` without unit tests: done (`mount.test.ts`).
- G2-25 a frame forced on every state change: done (`sameSceneInput` and the applied ref); Task 14 extends the comparison to agents.
- G2-26 `parseOklch` and `none`: done.
- G2-27 bloom threshold: done (0.7).
- G2-28 square particles: done (soft round motes, `world.test.ts`).
- G2-29 redundant 1023 px block: already gone; Task 17 rewrites the one that remains.
- G2-30 HUD servers gap: done.
- G2-31 two-row HUD at 1280 and 1366: done; Task 20 adds the regression test.
- G2-32 `.cs-context` dash transition: done (`stage.css` snaps it).
- G2-33 boot clock overshoot: done (capped in the frame).
- G2-34 jsdom `getContext` notices: done (`test-setup.ts` stub).
- G2-35 boot latch reset: done (`resetBootLatch`).
- G2-36 (part) OrbitControls' first update nudging the camera: the flight aims the camera itself until it lands (`cinematic.ts`).
- G2-37 HudCinematic checks a class, not clipping: axe covers the computed result.
- G2-38 budget script regexes: Vite always emits double-quoted root-absolute paths and the script fails loudly on a missing file; Task 18 adds the lazy-chunk guard instead.
- G2-39 telemetry tail-only scan: an optimization idea; the 200-message window keeps the scan cheap.
- G2-40 `FrameGuard.level` getter: cosmetic API.
- G2-41 the reviewer Leave list: accepted as written.
- G2-44 transition shorthand without a property false-fails: conservative by design.
